    <text xml:lang="sa-Latn-t-sa-Deva">
        <body>
            <div n="1" type="sthāna">
                <head xml:lang="en">[Sūtrasthāna 32-end]</head>
                <div n="32" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.1"><l><pb facs="225" n="141b"/> athātaḥ
                        svabhāvavipratipattim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.3"><l> svabhāvasiddhānāṃ śarīraikadeśānām
                        anyabhāvitvaṃ maraṇāya | tad yathā- śuklānāṃ kṛṣṇatvaṃ, kṛṣṇānāṃ
                        śuklatā, raktānām anyavarṇatvaṃ, sthirāṇāṃ mṛdutvaṃ , mṛdūnāṃ
                        sthiratā, calānāmacalatvaṃ, acalānāṃ calatā, pṛthūnāṃ
                        saṅkṣiptatvaṃ, saṅkṣiptānāṃ pṛthutā, dīrghāṇāṃ hrasvatvaṃ,
                        hrasvānāṃ dīrghatā, apatanadharmiṇāṃ patanadharmitvaṃ ,
                        patanadharmiṇām apatanadharmitvam aka<pb facs="226" n="142a"/>smāt, śaity auṣṇyasnaigdhyaraukṣyaprastambhavaivarṇyāvasādanaṃ
                        cāṅgānām ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.4"><l> svebhyaḥ sthānebhyaḥ
                        śarīraikadeśānāmavastrastotkṣiptabhrāntāvakṣiptapatitavimuktanirgatāntargatagurulaghutvāni,
                        pravālavarṇavyaṅgaprādurbhāvo vā'pyakasmāt, sirāṇāṃ ca darśanaṃ
                        lalāṭe, nāsāvaṃśe vā piḍakotpattiḥ , lalāṭe vā prabhātakāle
                        svedaḥ, netrarogādvinā vā'śrupravṛttiḥ, gomayacūrṇaprakāśasya vā
                        rajaso darśanamuttamāṅge nilayanaṃ vā kapotakaṅkakākaprabhṛtīnāṃ,
                        mūtrapurīṣavṛddhirabhuñjānānāṃ, tatpraṇāśo vā bhuñjānānāṃ,
                        stanamūlahṛdayor aḥsu ca śūlotpattayaḥ, madhye śūnatvamanteṣu
                        parimlāyitvaṃ viparyayo vā, tathā'rdhāṅge śvayathuḥ śoṣo
                        'ṅgapakṣayor vā, naṣṭahīnavikalavikṛtasvaratā vā,
                        vivarṇapuṣpaprādurbhāvo vā dantamukhanakhaśarīreṣu, yasya vā'psu
                        kaphapurīṣaretāṃsi nimajjanti, yasya vā dṛṣṭimaṇḍale
                        bhinnavikṛtāni rūpāṇyālokyante, snehābhyaktakeśāṅga iva yo bhāti,
                        yaś ca durbalo bhaktadveṣātisārābhyāṃ pīḍyate, kāsamānaś ca
                        tṛṣṇābhibhūtaḥ, kṣīṇaśchardibhaktadveṣayuktaḥ
                        saphenapūyarudhirodvāmī hatasvaraḥ śūlābhipannaś ca manuṣyaḥ,
                        śūnakaracaraṇavadanaḥkṣīṇo 'nnadveṣī srastapiṇḍikāṃsapāṇipādo
                        jvarakāsābhibhūtaḥ, yas tu pūrvāhṇe bhuktamaparāhṇe
                        chardayatyavidagdhamatisāryate vā sa śvāsānmriyate,
                        bastavadvilapan yaś ca bhūmau patati srastamuṣkaḥ, stabdhameḍhro
                        bhagnagrīvaḥ pranaṣṭamehanaś ca manuṣyaḥ, prāgviśuṣyamāṇāhṛdaya
                        ārdraśarīraḥ, yaś ca loṣṭaṃ loṣṭenābhihanti kāṣṭhaṃ kāṣṭhena,
                        tṛṇāni vā chinatti, adharoṣṭhaṃ daśati, uttaroṣṭhaṃ vā leḍhi,
                        āluñcati vā karṇau keśāṃś ca; devadvijagurusuhṛdvaidyāṃś ca
                        dveṣṭi, yasya vakrānuvakragā grahā garhitasthānagatāḥ pīḍayanti,
                        janmarkṣaṃ vā, yasyolkāśanibhyāmabhihanyate horā vā,
                        gṛhadāraśayanāsanayānavāhanamaṇiratnopakaraṇagarhitalakṣaṇanimittaprādurbhāvo
                        veti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="227" n="193b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.5"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         cikitsyamānaḥ samyak ca vikāro yo 'bhivardhate |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakṣīṇabalamāṃsasya lakṣaṇaṃ tadgatāyuṣaḥ ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.6"><l> nivartate mahāvyādhiḥ sahasā yasya dehinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         na cāhāraphalaṃ yasya dṛśyate sa vinaśyati ||6||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.7"><l> etāny ariṣṭarūpāṇi samyag budhyeta yo bhiṣak |
                        <caesura/>
                         sādhyāsādhyaparīkṣāyāṃ sa rājñaḥ sammato bhavet ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        svabhāvavipratipattirnāma dvātriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||32||</l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="33" type="adhyāya">

                    <pb facs="228" n="144a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.1"><l> athāto 'vāraṇīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.3"><l> upadravais tu ye juṣṭā vyādhayo yānty avāryatām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasāyanād vinā vatsa! tān śṛṇv ekamanā mama ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.4"><l> vātavyādhiḥ pramehaś ca kuṣṭham arśo
                        bhagandaram |
                        <caesura/>
                         aśmarī mūḍhagarbhaś ca tathaivodaram aṣṭamam ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.5"><l> aṣṭāv ete prakṛtyaiva duścikitsyā mahāgadāḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         prāṇamāṃsakṣayaḥ śoṣastṛṣṇā cchardirjvaras tathā ||5||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.6"><l> atīsāraś ca mūrcchā ca hikkā śvāsas tathaiva ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         etair upadravair juṣṭān sarvāneva vivarjayet ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.7"><l> śūnaṃ suptatvacaṃ bhagnaṃ kampādhmānanipīḍitam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         naraṃ rujārtamantaś ca vātavyādhir vināśayet ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.8"><l> yathoktopadravāviṣṭam atiprasrutam eva vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         piḍakāpīḍitaṃ gāḍhaṃ prameho hanti mānavam ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.9"><l> prabhinna prasrutāṅgaṃ ca raktanetraṃ
                        hatasvaram |
                        <caesura/>
                         pañcakarmaguṇātītaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ hantīha kuṣṭhinam ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.10"><l> tṛṣṇārocakaśūlārtam atiprasrutaśoṇitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         śophātīsārasaṃyuktam arśovyādhir vināśayet ||10|| </l></lg>


                   <pb facs="229" n="145a"/>
                     <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.11"><l> vātamūtrapurīṣāṇi krimayaḥ śukram eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhagandarāt prasravanti yasya taṃ parivarjayet ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.12"><l> praśūnanābhivṛṣaṇaṃ ruddhamūtraṃ ruganvitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         aśmarī kṣapayatyāśu sikatā śarkarānvitā ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.13"><l> garbhakoṣaparāsaṅgo makkallo yonisaṃvṛtiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         hanyāt striyaṃ mūḍhagarbhe yathoktāś cāpy upadravāḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.14"><l> pārśvabhaṅgānnavidveṣaśophātīsārapīḍitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         viriktaṃ pūryamāṇaṃ ca varjayed udarārditam ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.15"><l> yas tāmyati visañjñaś ca śete nipatito 'pi vā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītārdito 'ntaruṣṇaś ca jvareṇa mriyate naraḥ ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.16"><l> yo hṛṣṭaromā raktākṣo hṛdi saṅghātaśūlavān |
                        <caesura/>
                         nityaṃ vaktreṇa cocchvasyāttaṃ jvaro hanti mānavam ||16||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.17"><l> hikkāśvāsapipāsārtaṃ mūḍhaṃ vibhrāntalocanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         santatocchvāsinaṃ kṣīṇaṃ naraṃ kṣapayati jvaraḥ ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.18"><l> āvilākṣaṃ pratāmy antaṃ nidrāyuktam atīva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣīṇaśoṇitamāṃsaṃ ca naraṃ nāśayati jvaraḥ ||18|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.19"><l> śvāsaśūlapipāsārtaṃ kṣīṇaṃ jvaranipīḍitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         viśeṣeṇa naraṃ vṛddhamatīsāro vināśayet ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.20"><l> śuklākṣamannadveṣṭāramūrdhvaśvāsanipīḍitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛcchreṇa bahu mehantaṃ yakṣmā hantīha mānavam ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.21"><l> śvāsaśūlapipāsānnavidveṣagranthimūḍhatāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhavanti durbalatvaṃ ca gulmino mṛtyumeṣyataḥ ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.22"><l> ādhmātaṃ baddhaniṣyandaṃ chardihikkātṛḍanvitam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         rujāśvāsasamāviṣṭaṃ vidradhirnāśayennaram ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.23"><l> pāṇḍudantanakho yaś ca pāṇḍunetraś ca mānavaḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pāṇḍusaṅghātadarśī ca pāṇḍurogī vinaśyati ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="230" n="149a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.24"><l> lohitaṃ chardayed yas tu bahuśo lohitekṣaṇaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         raktānāṃ ca diśāṃ draṣṭā raktapittī vinaśyati ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.25"><l> avāṅmukhastūnmukho vā kṣīṇamāṃsabalo naraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         jāgariṣṇur asandeham unmādena vinaśyati ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.26"><l> bahuśo 'pasmarantaṃ tu prakṣīṇaṃ calitabhruvam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         netrābhyāṃ ca vikurvāṇam apasmāro vināśayet ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        'vāraṇīyo nāma trayastriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||33|| </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="34" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.1"><l> athāto yuktasenīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.3"><l> nṛpater yuktasenasya parānabhijigīṣataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhiṣajā rakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ yathā tadupadekṣyate ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.4"><l> vijigīṣuḥ sahāmātyair yātrāyuktaḥ prayatnataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         rakṣitavyo viśeṣeṇa viṣād eva narādhipaḥ ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.5"><l> panthānam udakaṃ chāyāṃ bhaktaṃ yavasam
                        indhanam | dūṣayantyarayastac ca jānīyācchodhayettathā ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.6"><l> tasya liṅgaṃ cikitsā ca kalpasthāne pravakṣyate
                        <caesura/>
                         ekottaraṃ mṛtyuśatam atharvāṇaḥ pracakṣate ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.7"><l> tatraikaḥ kālasaṃyuktaḥ śeṣā āgantavaḥ smṛtāḥ
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="231" n="147a"/> doṣāgantujamṛtyubhyo rasamantraviśāradau
                        ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.8"><l> rakṣetāṃ nṛpatiṃ nityaṃ yattau vaidyapurohitau
                        <caesura/>
                         brahmā vedāṅgam aṣṭāṅgam āyurvedam abhāṣata ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.9"><l> purohitamate tasmād varteta bhiṣag ātmavān
                        <caesura/>
                         saṅkaraḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ praṇāśo dharmakarmaṇām ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.10"><l> prajānām api cocchittirnṛpavyasanahetutaḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣāṇāṃ nṛpāṇāṃ ca kevalaṃ tulyamūrtitā ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.11"><l> ājñā tyāgaḥ kṣamā dhair yaṃ vikramaś cāpy
                        amānuṣaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmād devam ivābhīkṣṇaṃ vāṅmanaḥkarmabhiḥ śubhaiḥ ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.12"><l> cintayen nṛpatiṃ vaidyaḥ śreyāṃsīcchan
                        vicakṣaṇaḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         skandhāvāre ca mahati rājagehādanantaram ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.13"><l> bhavetsannihito vaidyaḥ sarvopakaraṇānvitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tatrasthamenaṃ dhvajavadyaśaḥkhyātisamucchritam ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.14"><l> upasarpantyamohena viṣaśalyāmayārditāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         svatantrakuśalo 'nyeṣu śāstrārtheṣv abahiṣkṛtaḥ ||14||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.15"><l> vaidyo dhvaja ivābhāti nṛpatadvidyapūjitaḥ
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="232" n="148a"/> vaidyo vyādhyupasṛṣṭaś ca bheṣajaṃ
                        paricārakaḥ ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.16"><l> ete pādāścikitsāyāḥ karmasādhanahetavaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         guṇavadbhis tribhiḥpādaiś caturtho guṇavān bhiṣak ||16||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.17"><l> vyādhimalpena kālena mahāntam api sādhayet
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyahīnāstrayaḥ pādā guṇavanto 'pyapārthakāḥ ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.18"><l> udgātṛhotṛbrahmāṇo yathā'dhvaryuṃ vinā'dhvare
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyastu guṇavānekastārayed āturāṃ sadā ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.19"><l> plavaṃ pratitarair hīnaṃ karṇadhāra ivāmbhasi
                        <caesura/>
                         tattvād higataśāstrārtho dṛṣṭakarmā svayaṅkṛtī ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.20"><l> laghuhastaḥ śuciḥ śūraḥ sajjopaskarabheṣajaḥ |
                        pratyutpannamatirdhīmān vyavasāyī viśāradaḥ ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.21"><l> satyadharmaparo yaś ca sa bhiṣak pāda ucyate
                        <caesura/>
                         āyuṣmān sattvavān sādhyo dravyavānātmavānapi ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.22"><l> āstiko vaidyavākyastho vyādhitaḥ pāda ucyate
                        <caesura/>
                         praśastadeśasambhūtaṃ praśaste 'hani coddhṛtam ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.23"><l> yuktamātraṃ manaskāntaṃ gandhavarṇarasānvitam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         doṣaghnamaglānikaramavikāri viparyaye |
                        <caesura/>
                         samīkṣya dattaṃ kāle ca bheṣajaṃ pāda ucyate ||23||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.24"><l> snigdho 'jugupsurbalavān yukto vyādhitarakṣaṇe
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyavākyakṛdaśrāntaḥ pādaḥ paricaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.34.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        yuktasenīyo nāma catustriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||34|| </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="35" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.1"><l><pb facs="232" n="148b"/> athātaḥ
                        āturopakramaṇīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.3"><l> āturam upakramamāṇena bhiṣajā''yurādāveva
                        parīkṣitavyaṃ ; satyāyuṣi
                        vyādhyṛtvagnivayodehabalasattvasātmyaprakṛtibheṣajadeśān parīkṣeta
                        ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="233" n="149a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.4"><l> tatra
                        mahāpāṇipādapārśvapṛṣṭhastanāgradaśanavadanaskandhalalāṭaṃ
                        dīrghāṅguliparvocchvāsaprekṣaṇabāhuṃ, vistīrṇabhrūstanāntaroraskaṃ
                        hrasvajaṅghāmeḍhragrīvaṃ, gambhīrasattvasvaranābhim, anuccair
                        baddhastanam, upacitamahāromaśakarṇaṃ, paścān mastiṣkaṃ,
                        snātānuliptaṃ mūrdhānupūrvyā viśuṣyamāṇaśarīraṃ paścāc ca
                        viśuṣyamāṇahṛdayaṃ puruṣaṃ jānīyāddīrghāyuḥ khalv ayam iti | tam
                        ekāntenopakramet | ebhir lakṣaṇair viparītair alpāyuḥ; miśrair
                        madhyamāyur iti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.5"><l> bhavanti cātra |
                        <caesura/>
                         gūḍhasandhisirāsnāyuḥ saṃhatāṅgaḥ sthirendriyaḥ|
                        <caesura/>
                         uttarottarasukṣetro yaḥ sa dīrghāyur ucyate ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.6"><l> garbhāt prabhṛtyarogo yaḥ śanaiḥ samupacīyate |
                        śarīrajñānavijñānaiḥ sa dīrghāyuḥ samāsataḥ ||6||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.7"><l> madhyamasyāyuṣo jñānamata ūrdhvaṃ nibodha me |
                        <caesura/>
                         adhastād akṣayor yasya lekhāḥ syurvyaktamāyatāḥ ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.8"><l> dve vā tisro 'dhikā vā'pi pādau karṇau ca
                        māṃsalau |
                        <caesura/>
                         nāsāgram ūrdhvaṃ ca bhaved ūrdhvaṃ lekhāś ca pṛṣṭhataḥ ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.9"><l> yasya syus tasya paramam āyur bhavati saptatiḥ
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="234" n="150a"/>jaghany asyāyuṣo jñānam ata ūrdhvaṃ
                        nibodha me ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.10"><l> hrasvāni yasya parvāṇi sumahac cāpi mehanam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tathorasy avalīḍhāni na ca syāt pṛṣṭham āyatam ||10||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.11abcd"><l> ūrdhvaṃ ca śravaṇau sthānān nāsā coccā
                        śarīriṇaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         hasato jalpato vā'pi dantamāṃsaṃ pradṛśyate |</l></lg>
                     <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.11ef"><l> prekṣate yaś ca vibhrāntaṃ sa jīvet
                        pañcaviṃśatim ||11||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.12ab"><l> atha punarāyuṣo vijñānārtham
                        aṅgapratyaṅgapramāṇasārānupadekṣyāmaḥ | tatrāṅgāny
                        antarādhisakthibāhuśirāṃsi, tad avayavāḥ pratyaṅgānīti | tatra,
                        svair aṅgulaiḥ pādāṅguṣṭhapradeśinyau dvyaṅgulāyate ;
                        pradeśinyāstu madhyamānāmikākaniṣṭhikā yathottaraṃ
                        pañcamabhāgahīnāḥ; caturaṅgulāyate pañcāṅgulavistṛte</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.12cd"><l> prapadapādatale; pañcacaturaṅgulāyatavistṛtā
                        pārṣṇiḥ; caturdaśāṅgulāyataḥ pādaḥ; caturdaśāṅgulapariṇāhāni
                        pādagulphajaṅghājānumadhyāni; aṣṭādaśāṅgulā jaṅghā, jānūpariṣṭācca
                        dvātriṃśadaṅgulam, ete pañcāśat; jaṅghāyāmasamāvūrū; dvyaṅgulāni
                        vṛṣaṇacibukadaśananāsāpuṭabhāgakarṇamūlabhrūnayanāntarāṇi;
                        caturaṅgulāni
                        mehanavadanāntaranāsākarṇalalāṭagrīvocchrāyadṛṣṭyantarāṇi;
                        dvādaśāṅgulāni
                        bhagavistāramehananābhihṛdayagrīvāstanāntaramukhāyāmamaṇibandhaprakoṣṭhasthaulyāni;
                        indrabastipariṇāhāṃsapīṭhakūrparāntarāyāmaḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaḥ;
                        caturviṃśatyaṅgulo hastaḥ; dvātriṃśadaṅgulaparimāṇau bhujau;
                        dvātriṃśatpariṇāhāvūrū; maṇibandhakūrparāntaraṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ;
                        talaṃ ṣaṭcaturaṅgulāyāmavistāram;
                        aṅguṣṭhamūlapradeśinīśravaṇāpāṅgāntaramadhyamāṅgulyau pañcāṅgule;
                        ardhapañcāṅgule pradeśinyanāmike; sārdhatryaṅgulau
                        kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhau; caturviṃśativistārapariṇāhaṃ mukhagrīvaṃ;
                        tribhāgāṅgulavistārā nāsāpuṭamaryādā; nayanatribhāgapariṇāhā
                        tārakā; navamastārakāṃśo dṛṣṭiḥ;
                        keśāntamastakāntaramekādaśāṅgulaṃ; mastakādavaṭukeśānto
                        daśāṅgulaḥ; karṇāvaṭvantaraṃ caturdaśāṅgulaṃ; puruṣoraḥpra<pb facs="234" n="151b"/>māṇavistīrṇā strīśroṇiḥ;
                        aṣṭādaśāṅgulavistāramuraḥ; tatpramāṇā puruṣasya kaṭī;
                        saviṃśamaṅgulaśataṃ puruṣāyāma iti ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.13"><l> bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pañcaviṃśe tato varṣe pumān nārī tu ṣoḍaśe |
                        <caesura/>
                         samatvāgatavīryau tau jānīyāt kuśalo bhiṣak ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.14"><l> dehaḥ svair aṅgulair eṣa yathāvadanukīrtitaḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         yuktaḥ pramāṇenānena pumān vā yadi vā'ṅganā ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.15"><l> dīrghamāyuravāpnoti vittaṃ ca mahadṛcchati |
                        <caesura/>
                         madhyamaṃ madhyamair āyurvittaṃ hīnais tathā'varam ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="236" n="152a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.16ab"><l> atha sārān vakṣyāmaḥ -
                        smṛtibhaktiprajñāśaucaśauryopetaṃ kalyāṇābhiniveśaṃ sattvasāraṃ
                        vidyāt; snigdhasaṃhataśvetāsthidantanakhaṃ bahulakāmaprajaṃ
                        śukreṇa; akṛśam uttamabalaṃ snigdhagambhīrasvaraṃ
                        saubhāgyopapannaṃ mahānetraṃ ca majjñā; mahāśiraḥskandhaṃ
                        dṛḍhadantahanvasthinakhamasthibhiḥ; snigdhamūtrasvedasvaraṃ
                        bṛhaccharīramāyāsāsahiṣṇuṃ medasā; acchidragātraṃ gūḍhāsthisandhiṃ
                        māṃsopacitaṃ ca māṃsena; </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.16cd"><l>
                        snigdhatāmranakhanayanatālujihvauṣṭhapāṇipādatalaṃ raktena;
                        suprasannamṛdutvagromāṇaṃ tvaksāraṃ vidyāditi | eṣāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ
                        pradhānamāyuḥsaubhāgyayor iti ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.17"><l> viśeṣato 'ṅgapratyaṅgapramāṇād atha sārataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         parīkṣyāyuḥ sunipuṇo bhiṣak sidhyati karmasu ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.18"><l> vyādhiviśeṣās tu prāgabhihitāḥ; sarva evaite
                        trividhāḥ- sādhyā, yāpyāḥ, pratyākhyeyāś ca | tatraitān
                        bhūyastridhā parīkṣeta- kimayamaupasargikaḥ, prākkevalaḥ,
                        anyalakṣaṇa iti | tatra, aupasargiko nāma yaḥ pūrvotpannaṃ vyādhiṃ
                        jaghanyakālajāto vyādhirupasṛjati, sa tanmūla evopadravasañjñaḥ;
                        prākkevalo nāma yaḥ prāgevotpanno vyādhirapūrvarūpo 'nupadravaś
                        ca; anyalakṣaṇo nāma yo bhaviṣyadvyādhikhyāpakaḥ, sa
                        pūrvarūpasañjñaḥ | tatra, sopadravamanyonyāvirodhenopakrameta,
                        balavantam upadravaṃ vā; prākkevalaṃ yathāsvaṃ pratikurvīta;
                        anyalakṣaṇe tvād ivyādhau prayateta ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="237" n="153a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.19"><l> bhavati cātra |
                        <caesura/>
                         nāsti rogo vinā doṣair yasmāt tasmād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         anuktam api doṣāṇāṃ liṅgair vyādhim upācaret ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.20"><l> prāgabhihitā ṛtavaḥ ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.21"><l> śīte śītapratīkāram uṣṇe coṣṇanivāraṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛtvā kuryāt kriyāṃ prāptāṃ kriyākālaṃ na hāpayet ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.22"><l> aprāpte vā kriyākāle prāpte vā na kṛtā kriyā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kriyā hīnā'tiriktā vā sādhyeṣv api na sidhyati ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.23"><l> yā hy udīrṇaṃ śamayati nānyaṃ vyādhiṃ karoti
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         sā kriyā, na tu yā vyādhiṃ haratyanyamudīrayet ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.24"><l> prāgabhihito 'gnir annasya pācakaḥ | sa
                        caturvidho bhavati- doṣānabhipanna ekaḥ, vikriyām āpannas tri<pb facs="238" n="154a"/>vidho bhavati- viṣamo vātena, tīkṣṇaḥ
                        pittena, mandaḥ śleṣmaṇā, caturthaḥ samaḥ sarvasāmyāditi | tatra,
                        yo yathākālam upayuktamannaṃ samyak pacati sa samaḥ, samair
                        doṣaiḥ; yaḥ kadācit samyak pacati, kadācid
                        ādhmānaśūlodāvartātisārajaṭharagauravāntrakūjanapravāhaṇāni kṛtvā,
                        sa viṣamaḥ; yaḥ prabhūtam apy upayuktam annam āśu pacati sa
                        tīkṣṇaḥ, sa evābhivardhamāno 'tyagnirity ābhāṣyate, sa muhur muhuḥ
                        prabhūtam apy upayuktam annam āśutaraṃ pacati, pākānte ca
                        galatālvoṣṭhaśoṣadāhasantāpā ñjanayati; yas tv
                        alpamapyupayuktamudaraśirogauravakāsaśvāsaprasekacchardigātrasadanāni
                        kṛtvā mahatā kālena pacati, sa mandaḥ ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.25"><l> viṣamo vātajān rogāṃst īkṣṇaḥ pittanimittajān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         karotyagnis tathā mando vikārān kaphasambhavān ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.26"><l> tatra, same parirakṣaṇaṃ kurvīta; viṣame
                        snigdhāmlalavaṇaiḥ kriyāviśeṣaiḥ pratikurvīta; tīkṣṇe
                        madhurasnigdhaśītair virekaiś ca; evam evātyagnau, viśeṣeṇa
                        māhiṣaiś ca kṣīradadhisarpirbhiḥ; mande kaṭutiktakaṣāyair vamanaiś
                        ca ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.27"><l> jāṭharo bhagavān agnir īśvaro 'nnasya pācakaḥ
                        | saukṣmyādrasānādadāno vivektuṃ naiva śakyate ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.28"><l> prāṇāpānasamānais tu sarvataḥ pavanaistribhiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhmāyate pālyate cāpi svāṃ svāṃ gatim avasthitaiḥ ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="239" n="155a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.29"><l> vayas tu trividhaṃ- bālyaṃ, madhyaṃ, vṛddham
                        iti | tatronaṣoḍaśavarṣīyā bālāḥ | te trividhāḥ- kṣīrapāḥ,
                        kṣīrānnādā, annādā iti | teṣu saṃvatsaraparāḥ kṣīrapāḥ,
                        dvisaṃvatsaraparāḥ kṣīrānnādāḥ, parato 'nnādā iti |
                        ṣoḍaśasaptatyor antare madhyaṃ vayaḥ | tasya vikalpo- vṛddhiḥ,
                        yauvanaṃ, sampūrṇatā, parihāṇiriti | tatra, āviṃśatervṛddhiḥ,
                        ātriṃśato yauvanam, ācatvāriṃśataḥ
                        sarvadhātvindriyabalavīryasampūrṇatā, ata
                        ūrdhvamīṣatparihāṇiryāvat saptatiriti | saptaterūrdhvaṃ
                        kṣīyamāṇadhātvindriyabalavīryotsāhamahanyahani valīpalitakhālity
                        ajuṣṭaṃ kāsaśvāsaprabhṛtibhir upadravair abhibhūyamānaṃ
                        sarvakriyāsvasamarthaṃ jīrṇāgāramivābhivṛṣṭamavasīdantaṃ
                        vṛddhamācakṣate ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.30"><l> tatrottarottarāsu vayovasthāsūttarottarā
                        bheṣajamātrāviśeṣā bhavanti, ṛte ca parihāṇeḥ; tatrādyāpekṣayā
                        pratikurvīta ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.31"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         bāle vivardhate śleṣmā madhyame pittam eva tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūyiṣṭhaṃ vardhate vāyurvṛddhe tadvīkṣya yojayet ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.32"><l> agnikṣāravirekais tu bālavṛddhau vivarjayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tatsādhyeṣu vikāreṣu mṛdvīṃ kuryāt kriyāṃ śanaiḥ ||32|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.33"><l> dehaḥ sthūlaḥ, kṛśo, madhya, iti prāgupadiṣṭaḥ
                        ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.34"><l> karśayed bṛṃhayec cāpi sadā sthūlakṛśau narau
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         rakṣaṇaṃ caiva madhyasya kurvīta satataṃ bhiṣak ||34|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="240" n="156a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.35"><l> balam abhihitaguṇaṃ; daurbalyaṃ tu
                        svabhāvadoṣajarādibhir avekṣitavyam |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasmād balavataḥ sarvakriyāpravṛttis tasmād balam eva pradhānam
                        adhikaraṇānām ||35|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.36"><l> kecit kṛśāḥ prāṇavantaḥ sthūlāś cālpabalā
                        narāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt sthiratvaṃ vyāyāmair balaṃ vaidyaḥ pratarkayet ||36|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.37"><l> sattvaṃ tu vyasanābhyudayakriyādisthāneṣv
                        aviklavakaram ||37|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.38"><l> sattvavān sahate sarvaṃ saṃstabhyātmānamātmanā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         rājasaḥ stabhyamāno 'nyaiḥ sahate naiva tāmasaḥ ||38|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.39"><l> sātmyāni tu
                        deśakālajātyṛturogavyāyāmodakadivāsvapnarasaprabhṛtīni
                        prakṛtiviruddhāny api yāny abādhakarāṇi bhavanti ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.40"><l> yo rasaḥ kalpate yasya sukhāyaiva niṣevitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vyāyāmajātam anyad vā tat sātmyam iti nirdiśet ||40|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="241" n="157a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.41"><l> prakṛtiṃ bheṣajaṃ copariṣṭād vakṣyāmaḥ ||41|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.42"><l> deśas tv ānūpo jāṅgalaḥ sādhāraṇa iti | tatra,
                        bahūdakanimnonnatanadīvarṣagahano mṛduśītānilo
                        bahumahāparvatavṛkṣo mṛdusukumāropacitaśarīramanuṣyaprāyaḥ
                        kaphavātarogabhūyiṣṭhaś cānūpaḥ; ākāśasamaḥ
                        praviralālpakaṇṭakivṛkṣaprāyo 'lpavarṣaprasravaṇodapānodakaprāya
                        uṣṇadāruṇavātaḥ praviralālpaśailaḥ sthirakṛśaśarīramanuṣyaprāyo
                        vātapittarogabhūyiṣṭhaś ca jāṅgalaḥ; ubhayadeśalakṣaṇaḥ sādhāraṇa
                        iti ||42|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.43"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         samāḥ sādhāraṇe yasmācchītavarṣoṣmamārutāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         doṣāṇāṃ samatā jantostasmāt sādhāraṇo mataḥ ||43|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.44"><l> na tathā balavantaḥ syurjalajā vā sthalāhṛtāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         svadeśe nicitā doṣā anyasmin kopam āgatāḥ ||44|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.45"><l> ucite vartamānasya nāsti deśakṛtaṃ bhayam | ૉ
                        <caesura/>
                         āhārasvapnaceṣṭādau taddeśasya guṇe sati ||45||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.46"><l> deśaprakṛtisātmyartu viparīto 'cirotthitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sampattau bhiṣagādīnāṃ balasattvāyuṣāṃ tathā ||46||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.47"><l> kevalaḥ samadehāgneḥ sukhasādhyatamo gadaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ato 'nyathā tvasādhyaḥ syāt kṛcchro vyāmiśralakṣaṇaḥ ||47||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.48"><l> kriyāyās tu guṇālābhe kriyām anyāṃ prayojayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvasyāṃ śāntavegāyāṃ na kriyāsaṅkaro hitaḥ ||48|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="242" n="158a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.49"><l> guṇālābhe 'pi sapadi yadi saiva kriyā hitā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kartavyaiva tadā vyādhiḥ kṛcchrasādhyatamo yadi ||49|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.50"><l> ya evam enaṃ vidhim ekarūpaṃ bibharti
                        kālādivaśena dhīmān |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa mṛtyupāśāñ jagato gadaughāñ chinatti bhaiṣajyaparaśvadhena
                        ||50|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.35.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        āturopakramaṇīyo nāma pañcatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||35|| </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="36" type="adhyāya">
                    <!--<note xml:lang="en">There are large differences
                                    between the Ācārya 1931 and 1938 editions version of
                                    Cakrapāṇidatta's commentary on this adhyāya.</note>-->

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.1"><l> athāto bhūmipravibhāgīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.3"><l>
                        śvabhraśarkarāśmaviṣamavalmīkaśmaśānāghātanadevatāyatanasikatābhir
                        anupahatāmanūṣarāmabhaṅgurāmadūrodakāṃ snigdhāṃ prarohavatīṃ
                        mṛdvīṃ sthirāṃ samāṃ kṛṣṇāṃ gaurīṃ lohitāṃ vā bhūmim
                        auṣadhagrahaṇāya parīkṣeta | tasyāṃ jātamapi
                        kṛmiviṣaśastrātapapavanadahanatoyasambādhamārgair
                        anupahatamekarasaṃ puṣṭaṃ pṛthvavagāḍhamūlamudīcyāṃ
                        cauṣadhamādadītetyeṣa bhūmiparīkṣāviśeṣaḥ sāmānyaḥ ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.4"><l> viśeṣatas tu tatra, aśmavatī sthirā gurvī śyāmā
                        kṛṣṇā vā sthūlavṛkṣaśasyaprāyā svaguṇabhūyiṣṭhā; snigdhā
                        śītalā''sannodakā snigdhaśasyatṛṇakomalavṛkṣaprāyā
                        śuklā'mbuguṇabhūyiṣṭhā; nānāvarṇā laghvaśmavatī
                        praviralālpapāṇḍuvṛkṣaprarohā'gniguṇabhūyiṣṭhā; rūkṣā
                        bhasmarāsabhavarṇā
                        tanuvṛkṣālparasakoṭaravṛkṣaprāyā'nilaguṇabhūyiṣṭhā; mṛdvī samā
                        śvabhravatyavyaktarasajalā sarvato 'sāravṛkṣā
                        mahāparvatavṛkṣaprāyā śyāmā cākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhā ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.5"><l> atra kecid āhur ācāryāḥ-
                        prāvṛḍvarṣāśaraddhemantavasantagrīṣmeṣu yathāsaṅkhyaṃ
                        mūlapatratvakkṣīrasāraphalāny ādadīteti; tat tu na samyak,
                        saumyāgneyatvāj jagataḥ | saumyāny auṣadhāni saumyeṣv ṛtuṣv
                        ādadīta, āgneyāny āgneyeṣu; evam avyāpannaguṇāni bhavanti |
                        saumyāny auṣadhmani saumyeṣvṛtuṣu gṛhītāni somaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ
                        bhūmau jātāny atimadhurasnigdhaśītāni jāyante | etena śeṣaṃ
                        vyākhyātam ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="243" n="159a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.6"><l> tatra pṛthivyambuguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ bhūmau jātāni
                        virecanadravyāṇy ādadīta , agnyākāśamārutaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ
                        vamanadravyāṇi, ubhayaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyām ubhayato bhāgāni,
                        ākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ saṃśamanāni, evaṃ balavattarāṇi bhavanti
                        ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.7"><l> sarvāṇyeva cābhinavāni, anyatra
                        madhughṛtaguḍapippalīviḍaṅgebhyaḥ ||7||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.8"><l> viḍaṅgaṃ pippalī kṣaudraṃ sarpiś cāpy anavaṃ
                        hitam | śeṣam anyatv abhinavaṃ gṛhṇīyāddoṣavarjitam ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.9"><l> (sarvāṇyeva sakṣīrāṇi vīryavanti;) teṣāṃ
                        asampattāvatikrāntasaṃvatsarāṇy ādadīteti ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.10"><l> bhavanti cātra -
                        <caesura/>
                         gopālās tāpasā vyādhā ye cānye vanacāriṇaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mūlāhārāś ca ye tebhyo bheṣajavyaktir iṣyate ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.11"><l> sarvāvayavasādhyeṣu palāśalavaṇādiṣu |
                        vyavasthito na kālo 'sti tatra sarvo vidhīyate ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="244" n="160a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.12"><l> gandhavarṇarasopetā ṣaḍvidhā bhūmiriṣyate |
                        tasmād bhūmisvabhāvena bījinaḥ ṣaḍrasāyutāḥ ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.13"><l> avyaktaḥ kila toyasya raso niścayaniścitaḥ |
                        rasaḥ sa eva cāvyakto vyakto bhūmirasādbhavet ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.14"><l> sarvalakṣaṇasampannā bhūmiḥ sādhāraṇā smṛtā |
                        dravyāṇi yatra tatraiva tadguṇāni viśeṣataḥ ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.15"><l> vigandhenāparāmṛṣṭamavipannaṃ rasādibhiḥ |
                        navaṃ dravyaṃ purāṇaṃ vā grāhyamevaṃ vinirdiśet ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.16"><l> jaṅgamānāṃ vayaḥsthānāṃ raktaromanakhādikam |
                        kṣīramūtrapurīṣāṇi jīrṇāhāreṣu saṃharet ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.17"><l> plotamṛdbhāṇḍaphalakaśaṅkuvinyastabheṣajam |
                        praśastāyāṃ diśi śucau bheṣajāgāramiṣyate ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.36.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        bhūmipravibhāgīyo nāma ṣaṭtriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ ||36|| </l></lg>
                </div>
                <div n="37" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.1"><l> athāto miśrakam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.3"><l> mātuluṅgyagnimanthau ca bhadradāru mahauṣadham
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         ahiṃsrā caiva rāsnā ca pralepo vātaśophajit ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.4"><l> dūrvā ca nalamūlaṃ ca madhukaṃ candanaṃ tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītalāś ca gaṇāḥ sarve pralepaḥ pittaśophahṛt ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.5"><l> āgantuje raktaje ca hyeṣa eva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         vidhir viṣaghno viṣaje pittaghno 'pi hitas tathā ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.6"><l> ajagandhā'śvagandhā ca kālā saralayā saha |
                        <caesura/>
                         ekaiṣikājaśṛṅgī ca pralepaḥ śleṣmaśophahṛt ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.7"><l> ete vargās trayo lodhraṃ pathyā piṇḍītakāni ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         anantā ceti lepo 'yaṃ sānnipātikaśophahṛt ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="245" n="161a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.8"><l> snigdhāmlalavaṇo vāte koṣṇaḥ, śītaḥ payoyutaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pitte, coṣṇaḥ kaphe kṣāramūtrāḍhyastatpraśāntaye ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.9"><l> śaṇamūlakaśigrūṇāṃ phalāni tilasarṣapāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         saktavaḥ kiṇvamatasī dravyāṇyuṣṇāni pācanam ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.10"><l> cirabilvo 'gniko dantī citrako hayamārakaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kapotakaṅkagṛdhrāṇāṃ purīṣāṇi ca dāraṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣāradravyāṇi vā yāni kṣāro vā dāraṇaṃ param ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.11"><l> dravyāṇāṃ picchilānāṃ tu tvaṅmūlāni prapīḍanam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         yavagodhūmamāṣāṇāṃ cūrṇāni ca samāsataḥ ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.12"><l> śāṅkhinyaṅkoṭhasumanaḥkaravīrasuvarcalāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śodhanāni kaṣāyāṇi vargaś cāragvadhādikaḥ ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.13"><l> ajagandhā'jaśṛṅgī ca gavākṣī lāṅgalāhvayā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūtīkaś citrakaḥ pāṭhā viḍaṅgailāhareṇavaḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.14"><l> yavakṣāro lavaṇāni manaḥśilā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsīsaṃ trivṛtā dantī haritālaṃ surāṣṭrajā ||14||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.15"><l> saṃśodhanīnāṃ vartīnāṃ dravyāṇy etāni nirdiśet
                        <caesura/>
                         etair eva uṣadhaiḥ kuryāt kalkānapi ca śodhanān ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="246" n="162a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.16"><l> arkottamāṃ snuhīkṣīraṃ piṣṭvā kṣārottamāni ca
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         jātīmūlaṃ haridre dve kāsīsaṃ kaṭurohiṇīm ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.17"><l> pūrvoddiṣṭāni cānyāni kuryāt saṃśodhanaṃ
                        ghṛtam
                        <caesura/>
                         mayūrako rājavṛkṣo nimbaḥ kośātakī tilāḥ ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.18"><l> bṛhatī kaṇṭakārī ca haritālaṃ manaḥśilā |
                        <caesura/>
                         śodhanāni ca yojyāni taile dravyāṇi śodhane ||18||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.19"><l> kāsīse saindhave kiṇve vacāyāṃ rajanīdvaye |
                        <caesura/>
                         śodhanāṅgeṣu cānyeṣu cūrṇaṃ kurvīta śodhanam ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.20"><l> sālasārādisāreṣu paṭolatriphalāsu ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasakriyā vidhātavyā śodhanī śodhaneṣu ca ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.21"><l> śrīveṣṭake sarjarase sarale devadāruṇi |
                        <caesura/>
                         sāreṣv api ca kurvīta matimān vraṇadhūpanam ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="246" n="162b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.22"><l> kaṣāyāṇām anuṣṇānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ tvakṣu sādhitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śṛtaḥ śītakaṣāyo vā ropaṇārthaṃ praśasyate ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.23"><l> somāmṛtāśvagandhāsu kākolyādau gaṇe tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣīripraroheṣv api ca vartayo ropaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.24"><l> samaṅgā somasaralā somavalkaḥ sacandanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kākolyādiś ca kalkaḥ syāt praśasto vraṇaropaṇe ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.25"><l> pṛthakparṇyātmaguptā ca haridre mālatī sitā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kākolyādiś ca yojyaḥ syādbhiṣajā ropaṇe ghṛte ||25|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.26"><l> kālānusāryāguruṇī haridre devadāru ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         priyaṅgavaś ca rodhraṃ ca taile yojyāni ropaṇe ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.27"><l> kaṅgukā triphalā rodhraṃ kāsīsaṃ śravaṇāhvayā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhavāśvakarṇayos tvak ca ropaṇaṃ cūrṇamiṣyate ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.28"><l> priyaṅgukā sarjarasaḥ puṣpakāsīsam eva ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         tvakcūrṇaṃ dhavajaṃ caiva ropaṇārthaṃ praśasyate ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="247" n="163a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.29"><l> tvakṣu nyagrodhavargasya triphalāyās tathaiva
                        ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasakriyāṃ ropaṇārthe vidadhīta yathākramam ||29|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.30"><l> apāmārgo 'śvagandhā ca tālapatrī suvarcalā |
                        <caesura/>
                         utsādane praśasyante kākolyādiś ca yo gaṇaḥ ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.31"><l> kāsīsaṃ saindhavaṃ kiṇvaṃ kuruvindo manaḥśilā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kukkuṭāṇḍakapālāni sumanomukulāni ca ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.32"><l> phale śair īṣakārañje dhātucūrṇāni yāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         vraṇeṣūtsannamāṃseṣu praśastānyavasādane ||32||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.33"><l> samastaṃ vargamardhaṃ vā yathālābhamathāpi vā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         prayuñjīta bhiṣak prājño yathoddiṣṭeṣu karmasu ||33|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.37.trailer"><l> iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        miśrakādhyāyo nāma saptatriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||37|| </l></lg>
                </div>

                <div n="38" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.1"><l> athāto dravayasaṃgrahaṇīyam adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.3"><l> samāsena saptatriṃśaddravyagaṇā bhavanti ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="248" n="164a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.4"><l> tad yathā- vidārigandhā vidārī viśvadevā
                        sahadevā śvadaṃṣṭrā pṛthakparṇī śatāvarī sārivā kṛṣṇasārivā
                        jīvakarṣabhakau mahāsahā kṣudrasahā bṛhatyau punarnavair aṇḍo
                        haṃsapādī vṛścikālyṛṣabhī ceti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.5"><l> vidārīgandhādirayaṃ gaṇaḥ pittānilāpahaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śoṣagulmāṅgamardordhvaśvāsakāsavināśanaḥ ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.6"><l>
                        āragvadhamadanagopaghoṇṭākaṇṭakīkuṭajapāṭhāpāṭalāmūrvendrayavasaptaparṇanimbakuruṇṭakadāsīkuruṇṭaka-
                        guḍūcīcitrakaśārṅga(rṅge)ṣṭākarañjadvayapaṭolakirātatiktakāni
                        suṣavī ceti ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.7"><l> āragvadhādir ity eṣa gaṇaḥ śleṣmaviṣāpahaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mehakuṣṭhajvaravamīkaṇḍūghno vraṇaśodhanaḥ ||7||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.8"><l>
                        sālasārājakarṇakhadirakadarakālaskandhakramukabhūrjameṣaśṛṅgatiniśacandanakucandanaśiṃśapā-
                        śirīṣāsanadhavārjunatālaśākanaktamālapūtīkāśvakarṇāgurūṇi
                        kālīyakaṃ ceti ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.9"><l> sālasārādirity eṣa gaṇaḥ kuṣṭhavināśanaḥ |
                        mehapāṇḍvāmayaharaḥ kaphamedoviśoṣaṇaḥ ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.10"><l>
                        varuṇārtagalaśigrumadhuśigrutarkārīmeṣaśṛṅgīpūtīkanaktamālamoraṭāgnimanthasaireyakadvayabimbīvasukavasiracitraka-śatāvarībilvājaśṛṅgīdarbhā
                        bṛhatīdvayaṃ ceti ||10|| </l></lg>

                      <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.11"><l> varuṇādirgaṇo hyeṣa kaphamedonivāraṇaḥ |
                        vinihanti śiraḥśūlagulmābhyantaravidradhīn ||11||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.12"><l>
                        vīratarusahacaradvayadarbhavṛkṣādanīgundrānalakuśakāśāśmabhedakāgnimanthamoraṭāvasukavasirabhallūkakuraṇṭikendīvarakapotavaṅkāḥ
                        śvadaṃṣṭrā ceti ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.13"><l> vīratarvādirity eṣa gaṇo vātavikāranut |
                        <caesura/>
                         aśmarīśarkarāmūtrakṛcchrāghātarujāpahaḥ ||13||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.14"><l>
                        lodhrasāvaralodhrapalāśakuṭannaṭāśokaphañjīkaṭphalailavālukaśallakījiṅginīkadambasālāḥ
                        kadalī ceti ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.15"><l> eṣa rodhrādirity ukto medaḥkaphaharo gaṇaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yonidoṣaharaḥ stambhī varṇyo viṣavināśanaḥ ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.16"><l>
                        arkālarkakarañjadvayanāgadantīmayūrakabhārgīrāsnendrapuṣpīkṣudraśvetāmahāśvetāvṛścikālyalavaṇāstāpasavṛkṣaś
                        ceti ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.17"><l> arkādiko gaṇo hyeṣa kaphamedoviṣāpahaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṛmikuṣṭhapraśamano viśeṣād vraṇaśodhanaḥ ||17|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.18"><l> surasāśvetasurasāphaṇijjhakārjaka<pc> </pc>bhūstṛṇasugandhaka<pc> </pc>sumukhakālamālakuṭherakakāsamardakṣavakakharapuṣpā<pc> </pc>viḍaṅgakaṭphala- surasī<pc> </pc>nirguṇḍīkulāhalondurukarṇikāphañjīprācībalakākamācyo
                        viṣamuṣṭikaś ceti ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.19"><l> surasādirgaṇo hyeṣa kaphahṛt kṛmisūdanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pratiśyāyāruciśvāsakāsaghno vraṇaśodhanaḥ ||19||</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="250" n="166a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.20"><l>
                        muṣkakapalāśadhavacitrakamadanavṛkṣakaśiṃśapāvajravṛkṣāstriphalā
                        ceti ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.21"><l> muṣkakādirgaṇo hyeṣa medoghnaḥ śukradoṣahṛt |
                        <caesura/>
                         mehārśaḥpāṇḍurogāśmaśarkarānāśanaḥ paraḥ ||21||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.22"><l>
                        pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakaśṛṅgaveramaricahastipippalīhareṇukailājamodendrayavapāṭhājīrakasarṣapa-
                        mahānimbaphalahiṅgubhārgīmadhurasātiviṣāvacāviḍaṅgāni kaṭurohiṇī
                        ceti ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.38.23"><l> pippalyādiḥ kaphaharaḥ pratiśyāyānilārucīḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nihanyāddīpano gulmaśūlaghnaś cāmapācanaḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.1"><l><pb facs="254" n="170a"/> athātaḥ

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.3"><l>
                        madanakuṭajajīmūtakekṣvākudhāmārgavakṛtavedhanasarṣapaviḍaṅgapippalīkarañjaprapunnāḍa
                        kovidārakarbudārāriṣṭāśvagandhāvidulabandhujīvakaśvetāśaṇapuṣpībimbīvacāmṛgervāravaścitrā
                        cetyūrdhvabhāgaharāṇi | tatra, kovidārapūrvāṇāṃ phalāni,
                        kovidārādīnāṃ mūlāni ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.4"><l>
                        vivṛtāśyāmādantīdravantīsaptalāśaṅkhinīviṣāṇikāgavākṣīcchagalāntrīsnuksu
                        varṇakṣīrīcitrakakiṇihīkuśakāśatilvakakampillakaramyakapāṭalāpūgaharītakyā
                        malakabibhītakanīlinīcaturaṅgulair
                        aṇḍapūtīkamahāvṛkṣasaptacchadārkā jyotiṣmatī cetyadhobhāgaharāṇi |
                        tatra tilvakapūrvāṇāṃ mūlāni , tilvakādīnāṃ pāṭalāntānāṃ tvacaḥ,
                        kampillakaphalarajaḥ, pūgādīnāmeraṇḍāntānāṃ phalāni,
                        pūtīkāragvadhayoḥ patrāṇi, śeṣāṇāṃ kṣīrāṇīti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="255" n="171a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.5"><l> kośātakī saptalā śaṅkhinī devadālī kāravellikā
                        cety ubhayatobhāgaharāṇi | eṣāṃ svarasā iti ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.6"><l>
                        pippalīviḍaṅgāpāmārgaśigrusiddhārthakaśirīṣamaricakaravīrabimbīgirikarṇikākiṇihīvacājyotiṣmatī-
                        karañjārkālarkalaśunātiviṣāśṛṅgaveratālīśatamālasurasārjakeṅgudīmeṣaśṛṅgīmātuluṅgīmuraṅgīpīlu-

                        śirovirecanāni | tatra karavīrapūrvāṇāṃ phalāni,

                        tālīśādīnāmarjakāntānāṃ patrāṇi, iṅgudīmeṣaśṛṅgyostvacaḥ,

                <div n="40" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.1"><l> athāto dravyarasaguṇavīryavipākavijñānīyam
                        adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.3"><l> kecid ācāryā bruvate- dravyaṃ pradhānaṃ,
                        kasmāt? vyavasthitatvāt , iha khalu dravyaṃ vyavasthitaṃ na
                        rasādayaḥ, yathā āme phale ye rasādayaste pakve na santi; nity
                        atvāc ca, nityaṃ hi dravyam anityā guṇāḥ, yathā
                        kalkādipravibhāgaḥ, sa eva sampannarasagandho vyāpannarasagandho
                        vā bhavati; svajātyavasthānācca, yathā <pb facs="258" n="174a"/>hi
                        pārthivaṃ dravyamanyabhāvaṃ na gacchaty evaṃ śeṣāṇi;
                        pañcendriyagrahaṇāś ca, pañcabhir indriyair gṛhyate dravyaṃ na
                        rasādayaḥ; āśrayatvācca, dravyamāśritā rasādayaḥ;
                        ārambhasāmarthyācca, dravyāśrita ārambhaḥ, yathā-
                        ‘vidārigandhādimāhṛtya saṅkṣudya vipacet’ ity evam ādiṣu na
                        rasādiṣvārambhaḥ; śāstraprāmāṇyācca, śāstre hi dravyaṃ pradhānam
                        upadeśe yogānāṃ, yathā- ‘mātuluṅgāgnimanthau ca’ ity ādau na
                        rasādaya upadiśyante; kramāpekṣitatvācca rasādīnāṃ, rasādayo hi
                        dravyakramamapekṣante, yathā- taruṇe taruṇāḥ sampūrṇe sampūrṇā
                        iti; ekadeśasādhyatvācca, dravyāṇāmekadeśenāpi vyādhayaḥ
                        sādhyante, yathā- mahāvṛkṣakṣīreṇeti; tasmād dravyaṃ pradhānaṃ, na
                        rasādayaḥ, kasmāt? niravayavatvāt | dravyalakṣaṇaṃ tu
                        ‘kriyāguṇavat samavāyikāraṇam’ iti ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.4"><l> netyāhuranye , rasās tu pradhānaṃ; kasmāt?
                        āgamāt, āgamo hi śāstramucyate; śāstre hi rasā adhikṛtāḥ, yathā-
                        rasāyatta āhāra iti, tasmiṃs tu prāṇāḥ; upadeśācca, upadiśyante hi
                        rasāḥ, yathā- madhurāmlalavaṇā vātaṃ śamayanti; anumānācca, rasena
                        hy anumīyate dravyaṃ, yathā- madhuramiti; ṛṣivacanācca, ṛṣivacanaṃ
                        vedo yathā- kiñcidijyārthaṃ madhuramāharediti, tasmād rasāḥ
                        pradhānaṃ; raseṣu guṇasañjñā | rasalakṣaṇamanyatropadekṣyāmaḥ
                        ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.5"><l> nety āhur anye , vīryaṃ pradhānam iti | kasmāt?
                        tad vaśe<pb facs="259" n="175a"/>nauṣadhakarmaniṣpatteḥ |
                        ihauṣadhakarmāṇyūrdhvādhobhāgobhayabhāgasaṃśodhanasaṃśamanasaṅgrāhikāgnidīpanapīḍanalekhanabṛṃhaṇa-
                        rasāyanavājīkaraṇaśvayathukaravilayanadahanadāraṇamādanaprāṇaghnaviṣapraśamanādīni
                        vīryaprādhānyād bhavanti | tac ca vīryaṃ dvividham uṣṇaṃ śītaṃ ca,
                        agnīṣomīyatvāj jagataḥ | kecid aṣṭavidham āhuḥ- śītam uṣṇaṃ
                        snigdhaṃ rūkṣaṃ viśadaṃ picchilaṃ mṛdu tīkṣṇaṃ <pb facs="260" n="176a"/>ceti | etāni vīryāṇi
                        svabalaguṇotkarṣādrasamabhibhūyātmakarma kurvanti | yathā
                        tāvanmahatpañcamūlaṃ kaṣāyaṃ tiktānurasaṃ vātaṃ śamayati,
                        uṣṇavīryatvāt ; tathā kulatthaḥ kaṣāyaḥ, kaṭukaḥ palāṇḍuḥ,
                        snehabhāvācca; madhuraścekṣuraso vātaṃ vardhayati, śītavīryatvāt ;
                        kaṭukā pippalī pittaṃ śamayati, mṛduśītavīryatvāt ; amlamāmalakaṃ
                        lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ ca; tiktā kākamācī pittaṃ vardhayati,
                        uṣṇavīryatvāt , madhurā matsyāś ca; kaṭukaṃ mūlakaṃ śleṣmāṇaṃ
                        vardhayati, snigdhavīryatvāt ; amlaṃ kapitthaṃ śleṣmāṇāṃ śamayati,
                        rūkṣavīryatvāt , madhuraṃ kṣaudraṃ ca; tad etan nidarśanamātram
                        uktam ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.6"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         ye rasā vātaśamanā bhavanti yadi teṣu vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         raukṣyalāghavaśaity āni na te hanyuḥ samīraṇam ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.7"><l> ye rasāḥ pittaśamanā bhavanti yadi teṣu vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         taikṣṇyauṣṇyalaghutāś caiva na te tat karmakāriṇaḥ ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.8"><l> ye rasāḥ śleṣmaśamanā bhavanti yadi teṣu vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         snehagauravaśaityāni na te tatkarmakāriṇaḥ ||8||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.9"><l> tasmād vīryaṃ pradhānam iti ||9||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.10"><l> nety āhur anye , vipākaḥ pradhānam iti |
                        kasmāt? <pb facs="261" n="177a"/>samyaṅmithyāvipakvatvāt ; iha
                        sarvadravāṇy abhyavahṛtāni samyaṅmithyāvipakvāni guṇaṃ doṣaṃ vā
                        janayanti | <pb facs="262" n="178a"/> tatrāhur anye - prati rasaṃ
                        pāka iti | kecit trividham icchanti- madhuramamlaṃ kaṭukaṃ ceti |
                        tat tu na samyak, bhūtaguṇādāmāccānyo 'mlo vipāko nāsti; pittaṃ hi
                        vidagdhamamlatām upaity āgneyatvāt ; yady evaṃ lavaṇo 'pyanyaḥ
                        pāko bhaviṣyati, śleṣmā hi vidagdho lavaṇatām upaitīti | madhuro
                        madhurasyāmlo 'mlasyaivaṃ sarveṣām iti kecid āhuḥ; dṛṣṭāntaṃ
                        copadiśanti- yathā tāvat kṣīram ukhāgataṃ pacyamānaṃ madhuram eva
                        syāt tathā śāliyavamudgādayaḥ prakīrṇāḥ svabhāvam uttarakāle 'pi
                            <pb facs="263" n="179a"/>na parityajanti tadvad iti | kecid
                        vadanti- abalavanto balavatāṃ vaśam āyāntīti | evam anavasthitiḥ,
                        tasmād asiddhānta eṣaḥ | āgame hi dvividha eva pāko madhuraḥ
                        kaṭukaś ca | tayor madhurākhyo guruḥ, kaṭukākhyo laghur iti |
                        tatra pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśānāṃ dvaividhyaṃ bhavati guṇasādharmyād
                        gurutā laghutā ca; pṛthivyāpaś ca gurvyaḥ, śeṣāṇi laghūni; tasmād
                        dvividha eva pāka iti ||10|| </l></lg>

                      <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.11"><l> bhavanti cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         dravyeṣu pacyamāneṣu yeṣv ambupṛthivīguṇāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirvartante 'dhikās tatra pāko madhura ucyate ||11||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.12"><l> tejo 'nilākāśaguṇāḥ pacyamāneṣu yeṣu tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         nirvartante 'dhikās tatra pākaḥ kaṭuka ucyate ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.13"><l> pṛthaktvadarśināmeṣa vādināṃ vādasaṅgrahaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         caturṇāmapi sāmagryam icchanty atra vipaścitaḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.14"><l> tad dravyam ātmanā kiñcit kiñcid vīryeṇa
                        sevitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kiñcid rasavipākābhyāṃ doṣaṃ hanti karoti vā ||14|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="264" n="180a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.15"><l> pāko nāsti vinā vīryādvīryaṃ nāsti vinā rasāt
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         raso nāsti vinā dravyāddravyaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ smṛtam ||15|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.16"><l> janma tu dravyarasayor anyonyāpekṣikaṃ smṛtam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         anyonyāpekṣikaṃ janma yathā syād dehadehinoḥ ||16|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.17"><l> vīryasañjñā guṇā ye 'ṣṭau te 'pi dravyāśrayāḥ
                        smṛtāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         raseṣu na bhavanty ete nirguṇās tu guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||17||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.18"><l> dravye dravyāṇi yasmāddhi vipacyante na
                        ṣaḍrasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śreṣṭhaṃ dravyamato jñeyaṃ, śeṣā bhāvās tad āśrayāḥ ||18|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.19"><l> amīmāṃsyāny acintyāni prasiddhāni svabhāvataḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         āgamenopayojyāni bheṣajāni vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||19|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="265" n="181a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.20"><l> pratyakṣalakṣaṇaphalāḥ prasiddhāś ca
                        svabhāvataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         nauṣadhīrhetubhir vidvān parīkṣeta kadācana ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.21"><l> sahasreṇāpi hetūnāṃ nāmbaṣṭhādir virecayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt tiṣṭhet tu matimān āgame na tu hetuṣu ||21|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.40.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        dravyaguṇarasavīryavipākavijñānīyo nāma catvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ

                        'mlo lavaṇaḥ kaṭukastiktaḥ kaṣāya iti | te ca bhūyaḥ
                        parasparasaṃsargāt triṣaṣṭidhā bhidyante | tatra,

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.5"><l> tatra vāyor ātmaivātmā, pittamāgneyaṃ, śleṣmā
                        saumya iti ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.6"><l> ta ete rasāḥ svayonivardhanā,
                        anyayonipraśamanāś ca ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.7"><l> kecid āhuḥ agnīṣomīyatvāj jagato rasā

                        kaṭvamlalavaṇā āgneyāḥ | tatra madhurāmlalavaṇāḥ snigdhā guravaś
                        ca, kaṭutiktakaṣāyā rūkṣā laghavaś ca; saumyāḥ śītāḥ, āgneyā uṣṇāḥ
                        ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.1"><l> tatra
                        śaityaraukṣyalāghavavaiśadyavaiṣṭambhyaguṇalakṣaṇo vāyuḥ, tasya
                        samānayoniḥ kaṣāyo rasaḥ; so 'sya śaityāc chaityaṃ vardhayati,
                        raukṣyād raukṣyaṃ, lāghavāl lāghavaṃ, vaiśadyād vaiśadyaṃ,
                        vaiṣṭambhyād vaiṣṭambhyam iti; (1) |8|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.2"><l>
                        auṣṇyataikṣṇyaraukṣyalāghavavaiśadyaguṇalakṣaṇaṃ pittaṃ, tasya
                        samānayoniḥ kaṭuko rasaḥ; so 'sya auṣṇyā<pb facs="269" n="184a"/>d
                        auṣṇyaṃ vardhayati, taikṣṇyāt taikṣṇyaṃ, raukṣyād raukṣyaṃ,
                        lāghavāl lāghavaṃ, vaiśadyād vaiśadyam iti (2) |8| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.3"><l>
                        mādhuryasnehagauravaśaityapaicchilyaguṇalakṣaṇaḥ śleṣmā; tasya
                        samānayonir madhuro rasaḥ, so 'sya mādhuryānmādhuryaṃ vardhayati,
                        snehāt snehaṃ, gauravād gauravaṃ, śaityāc chaity aṃ, paicchilyāt
                        paicchilyam iti (3) ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.4"><l> tasya punaranyayoniḥ kaṭuko rasaḥ; sa
                        śleṣmaṇaḥ pratyanīkatvāt kaṭukatvānmādhuryamabhibhavati, raukṣyāt
                        snehaṃ, lāghavād gauravam, auṣṇyāc chaityaṃ, vaiśadyāt paicchilyam
                        iti | tad etan nidarśanamātram uktaṃ bhavati (4) ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.9"><l> rasalakṣaṇam ata ūrdhvaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ- tatra, yaḥ
                        paritoṣamutpādayati prahlādayati tarpayati jīvayati mukhopalepaṃ
                        janayati śleṣmāṇaṃ cābhivardhayati sa madhuraḥ; yo dantaharṣam
                        utpādayati mukhāsrāvaṃ janayati śraddhāṃ cotpādayati so 'mlaḥ; yo
                        bhaktarucim utpādayati kaphaprasekaṃ janayati mārdavaṃ cāpādayati
                        sa lavaṇaḥ; yo jihvāgraṃ bādhate udvegaṃ janayati śiro gṛhṇīte
                        nāsikāṃ srāvayati sa kaṭukaḥ; yo gale coṣamutpādayati
                        mukhavaiśadyaṃ janayati bhaktaruciṃ cāpādayati harṣaṃ ca sa
                        tiktaḥ; yo vaktraṃ pariśoṣayati jihvāṃ stambhayati kaṇṭhaṃ
                        badhnāti hṛdayaṃ karṣati pīḍayati ca sa kaṣāya iti ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.1"><l> rasaguṇān ata ūrdhvaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ- tatra,
                        madhuro raso rasaraktamāṃsamedo 'sthimajjaujaḥśukrastanyavardhanaś
                        cakṣuṣyaḥ keśyo varṇyo balakṛtsandhānaḥ śoṇitarasaprasādano
                        bālavṛddhakṣatakṣīṇahitaḥ
                        ṣaṭpadapipīlikānāmiṣṭatamastṛṣṇāmūrcchādāhapraśamanaḥ
                        ṣaḍindriyaprasādanaḥ kṛmikaphakaraś ceti; sa evaṅguṇo 'pyeka
                        evātyartham āsevyamānaḥ
                        kāsaśvāsālasakavamathuvadanamādhuryasvaropaghātakṛmigalagaṇḍānāpādayati
                        tathā'rbudaślīpadabastigudopalepābhiṣyandaprabhṛtī ñjanayati(1)
                        |10| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="269" n="185b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.2"><l> amlo jaraṇaḥ pācano dīpanaḥ pavananigrahaṇo
                        'nulomanaḥ koṣṭhavidāhī bahiḥśītaḥ kledanaḥ prāyaśo hy adyaśceti;
                        sa evaṅguṇo 'pyeka evātyartham upasevyamāno
                        dantaharṣanayanasammīlanaromasaṃvejanakaphavilayanaśarīraśaithilyānyāpādayati,
                        tathā
                        kṣatābhihatadagdhadaṣṭabhagnarugṇaśūnapracyutāvamūtritavisarpitacchinnabhinnaviddhotpiṣṭādīni
                        pācayatyāgneyasvabhāvāt paridahati kaṇṭhamuro hṛdayaṃ ceti(2)
                        |10|</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.3"><l> lavaṇaḥ saṃśodhanaḥ pācano viśleṣaṇaḥ
                        kledanaḥ śaithilyakṛduṣṇaḥ sarvarasapratyanīko mārgaviśodhanaḥ
                        sarvaśarīrāvayavamārdavakaraśceti; sa evaṅguṇo 'pyeka
                        evātyarthamāsevyamāno
                        gātrakaṇḍūkoṭhaśophavaivarṇyapuṃstvopaghātendriyopatāpamukhākṣipākaraktapittavātaśoṇitāmlīkāprabhṛtīn
                        āpādayati(3) |10| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.4"><l> kaṭuko dīpanaḥ pācano rocanaḥ śodhanaḥ
                        sthaulyālasyakaphakṛmiviṣakuṣṭhakaṇḍūpraśamanaḥ
                        sandhibandhavicchedano 'vasādanaḥ stanyaśukramedasām upahantā
                        ceti; sa evaṅguṇo 'pyeka evātyartham upasevyamāno
                        bhramamadagalatālvoṣṭhaśoṣadāhasantāpabalavighātakampatodabhedakṛt
                        karacaraṇapārśvapṛṣṭhaprabhṛtiṣu ca vātaśūlān āpādayati(4)
                        |10|</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="270" n="189a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.5"><l> tiktaśchedano rocano dīpanaḥ śodhanaḥ
                        kaṇḍūkoṭhatṛṣṇāmūrcchājvarapraśamanaḥ stanyaśodhano
                        viṇmūtrakledamedovasāpūyopaśoṣaṇaśceti; sa evaṅguṇo 'pyeka
                        evātyartham upasevyamāno
                        gātramanyāstambhākṣepakārditaśiraḥśūlabhramatodabhedacchedāsyavair
                        asyāny āpādayati(5) |10| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.6"><l> kaṣāyaḥ saṅgrāhako ropaṇaḥ stambhanaḥ
                        śodhano lekhanaḥ śoṣaṇaḥ pīḍanaḥ kledopaśoṣaṇaś ceti; sa evaṅguṇo
                        'pyeka evātyartham upasevyamāno
                        hṛtpīḍāsyaśoṣodarādhmānavākyagrahamanyāstambha-
                        gātrasphuraṇacumucumāyanākuñcanākṣepaṇaprabhṛtī ñjanayati ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.11"><l> ataḥ sarveṣām eva dravyāṇyupadekṣyāmaḥ | tad
                        yathā- kākolyādiḥ
                        kṣīraghṛtavasāmajjaśāliṣaṣṭikayavagodhūmamāṣaśṛṅgāṭakakaserukatrapusair
                        vārukarkārukālābūkālindakataka-
                        giloḍyapriyālapuṣkarabījakāśmaryamadhūkadrākṣākharjūrarājādanatālanālikerekṣuvikārabalātibalātmaguptā-
                        vidārīpayasyāgokṣurakakṣīramoraṭamadhūlikākūṣmāṇḍaprabhṛtīni
                        samāsena madhuro vargaḥ;
                        dāḍimāmalakamātuluṅgāmrātakakapitthakaramardabadarakolaprācīnāmalakatintiḍīkakośāmrakabhavyapārāvata-
                        vetraphalalakucāmlavetasadantaśaṭhadadhitakrasurāśuktasauvīrakatuṣodakadhānyāmlaprabhṛtīni
                        samāsenāmlo vargaḥ;
                        saindhavasauvarcalaviḍapākyaromakasāmudrakapaktrimayavakṣāroṣaraprasūtasuvarcikāprabhṛtīni
                        samāsena lavaṇo vargaḥ; pippalyādiḥ surasādiḥ
                        śigrumadhuśigrumūlakalaśunasumukhaśītaśivakuṣṭhadevadāruhareṇukāvālgujaphala-
                        caṇḍāguggulumustalāṅgalakīśukanāsāpīluprabhṛtīni sālasārādiś ca
                        prāyaśaḥ kaṭuko vargaḥ;
                        āragvadhādirguḍūcyādirmaṇḍūkaparṇīvetrakarīraharidrādvayendrayavavaruṇasvādukaṇṭakasaptaparṇabṛhatīdvayaśaṅkhinīdravantī-
                        trivṛtkṛtavedhanakarkoṭakakāravellavārtākakarīrakaravīrasumanaḥśaṅkhapuṣpyapāmārgatrāyamāṇāśokarohiṇīvaijayantī-
                        suvarcalāpunarnavāvṛścikālījyotiṣmatīprabhṛtīni samāsena tikto
                        vargaḥ; nyagrodhādirambaṣṭhādiḥ priyaṅgvādī
                        rodhrādistriphalāśallakījambvāmrabakulatindukaphalāni
                        katakaśākaphalapāṣāṇabhedakavanaspatiphalāni sālasārādiś ca
                        prāyaśaḥ
                        kuruvakakovidārakajīvantīcillīpālaṅkyāsuniṣaṇṇakaprabhṛtīni
                        varakādayo mudgādayaś ca samāsena kaṣāyo vargaḥ ||11|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="271" n="187a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.12"><l> tatraiteṣāṃ rasānāṃ saṃyogāstriṣaṣṭirbhavanti
                        | tad yathā- pañcadaśa dvikāḥ, viṃśatistrikāḥ, pañcadaśa catuṣkāḥ,
                        ṣaṭ pañcakāḥ, ekaśaḥ ṣaḍrasāḥ, ekaḥ ṣaṭka iti | teṣāṃ anyatra
                        prayojanāni vakṣyāmaḥ ||12|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.13"><l> bhavati cātra
                        <caesura/>
                         jagdhāḥ ṣaḍadhigacchanti balino vaśyatāṃ rasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathā prakupitā doṣā vaśaṃ yānti balīyasaḥ ||13|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        rasaviśeṣavijñānīyo nāma dvicatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||42|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.part1"><l> vamanadravyāṇāṃ phalādīnāṃ madanaphalāni

                        prāṅmukhamāturaṃ pāyayed anena mantreṇābhimantrya-</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.part2"><l> viśeṣeṇa

                        santānikāṃ kṣaudrayuktāṃ , madanaphala<pb facs="272" n="188a"/>majjasiddhaṃ vā payaḥ, madanaphalamajjasiddhena vā payasā
