                        <caesura/>
                         tailabhṛṣṭān rasān amlaphalair āvāpya sādhayet ||20|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.21"><l> ghanībhūtaṃ
                        trisaugandhyatrivṛtkṣaudrasamanvitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         lehyam etat kaphaprāyaiḥ sukumārair virecanam ||21||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.22"><l> nīlītulyaṃ tvagelaṃ ca tais trivṛt sasitopalā
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇaṃ santarpaṇaṃ kṣaudraphalāmlaṃ sannipātanut ||22|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.23"><l> trivṛcchyāmāsitākṛṣṇātriphalāmākṣikaiḥ samaiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         modakāḥ sannipātordhvaraktapittajvarāpahāḥ ||23|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.24"><l> trivṛdbhāgāstrayaḥ proktāstriphalā tatsamā
                        tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣārakṛṣṇāviḍaṅgāni sañcūrṇya madhusarpiṣā ||24|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="276" n="192a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.25"><l> lihyād guḍena guṭikāḥ kṛtvā vā'pyatha
                        bhakṣayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaphavātakṛtān gulmān plīhodarahalīmakān ||25||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.26"><l> hanty anyān api cāpy etan nirapāyaṃ virecanam
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇaṃ śyāmā trivṛnnīlī kaṭvī mustā durālabhā ||26|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.27"><l> cavyendrabījaṃ triphalā sarpirmāṃsarasāmbubhiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pītaṃ virecanaṃ taddhi rūkṣāṇām api śasyate ||27|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.28"><l> vairecanikaniḥkvāthabhāgāḥ śītāstrayo matāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dvau phāṇitasya tac cāpi punar agnāv adhiśrayet ||28|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.29"><l> tat sādhusiddhaṃ vijñāya śītaṃ kṛtvā
                        nidhāpayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         kalase kṛtasaṃskāre vibhajya rtū himāhimau ||29||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.30"><l> māsād ūrdhvaṃ jātarasaṃ madhugandhaṃ varāsavam
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         pibed asāv eva vidhiḥ kṣāramūtrāsaveṣv api ||30|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.31"><l> vairecanikamūlānāṃ kvāthe māṣān subhāvitān |
                        <caesura/>
                         sudhautāṃs tat kaṣāyeṇa śālīnāṃ cāpi taṇḍulān ||31|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.32"><l> avakṣudyaikataḥ piṇḍān kṛtvā śuṣkān sucūrṇitān
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         śālitaṇḍulacūrṇaṃ ca tat kaṣāyoṣmasādhitam ||32||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.33"><l> tasya piṣṭasya bhāgāṃstrīn
                        kiṇvabhāgavimiśritān |
                        <caesura/>
                         maṇḍodakārthe kvāthaṃ ca dadyāt tat sarvam ekataḥ ||33||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.34"><l> nidadhyāt kalase tāṃ tu surāṃ jātarasāṃ pibet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         eṣa eva surākalpo vamaneṣv api kīrtitaḥ ||34||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.35"><l> mūlāni trivṛdādīnāṃ prathamasya gaṇasya ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         mahataḥ pañcamūlasya mūrvāśārṅgaṣṭayor api ||35|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.36"><l> sudhāṃ haimavatīṃ caiva triphalātiviṣe vacām |
                        <caesura/>
                         saṃhṛtyaitāni bhāgau dvau kārayed ekam etayoḥ ||36|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.37"><l> kuryān niḥkvātham ekasminn ekasmiṃś cūrṇam eva
                        tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         kṣuṇṇāṃs tasmiṃs tu niḥkvāthe bhāvayed bahuśo yavān ||37|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.38"><l> śuṣkāṇāṃ mṛdubhṛṣṭānāṃ teṣāṃ bhāgāstrayo matāḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         caturthaṃ bhāgamāvāpya cūrṇānāmatra kīrtitam ||38|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.39"><l> prakṣipya kalase samyak tatastaṃ tadanantaram
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         teṣāṃ eva kaṣāyeṇa śītalena suyojitam ||39|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.40"><l> pūrvavat sannidadhyāt tu jñeyaṃ sauvīrakaṃ hi
                        tat |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvoktaṃ vargam āhṛtya dvidhā kṛtvaikam etayoḥ ||40|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.41"><l> bhāgaṃ saṅkṣudya saṃsṛjya yavaiḥ
                        sthālyāmadhiśrayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         ajaśṛṅgyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa tamabhyāsicya sādhayet ||41|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="277" n="193a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.42"><l> susiddhāṃś cāvatāryaitān auṣadhibhyo vivecayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vimṛdya satuṣān samyak tatastān pūrvavan mitān ||42||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.43"><l> pūrvoktauṣadhabhāgasya cūrṇaṃ dattvā tu
                        pūrvavat |
                        <caesura/>
                         tenaiva saha yūṣeṇa kalase pūrvavat kṣipet ||43|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.44"><l> jñātvā jātarasaṃ cāpi tattuṣodakam ādiśet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tuṣāmbusauvīrakayor vidhireṣa prakīrtitaḥ ||44||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.45"><l> ṣaḍrātrāt saptarātrādvā te ca peye prakīrtite
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         vairecaneṣu sarvyeṣu trivṛnmūlavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||45|| </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.46"><l> dantīdravantyor mūlāni viśeṣān mṛtkuśāntare |
                        <caesura/>
                         pippalīkṣaudrayuktāni svinnāny uddhṛtya śoṣayet ||46|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.47"><l> tatas trivṛdvidhānena yojayecchleṣmapittayoḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         tayoḥ kalkakaṣāyābhyāṃ cakratailaṃ vipācayet ||47|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.48"><l> sarpiś ca pakvaṃ vīsarpakakṣādāhālajīrjayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         mehagulmānilaśleṣmavibandhāṃstailam eva ca ||48||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.49"><l> catuḥsnehaṃ śakṛcchukravātasaṃrodhajā rujaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dantīdravantīmaricakanakāhvayavāsakaiḥ ||49|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.50"><l> viśvabheṣajamṛdvīkācitrakair mūtrabhāvitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         saptāhaṃ sarpiṣā cūrṇaṃ yojyam etad virecanam ||50||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.51"><l> jīrṇe santarpaṇaṃ kṣaudraṃ
                        pittaśleṣmarujāpaham |
                        <caesura/>
                         ajīrṇapārśvarukpāṇḍuplīhodaranibarhaṇam ||51||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.52"><l> guḍasyāṣṭapale pathyā viṃśatiḥ syuḥ palaṃ
                        palam |
                        <caesura/>
                         dantīcitrakayoḥ karṣau pippalītrivṛtordaśa ||52|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.53"><l> kṛtvaitān modakān ekaṃ daśame daśame 'hani |
                        <caesura/>
                         tataḥ khāded uṣṇatoyasevī niryantraṇās tv ime ||53|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.54ab"><l> doṣaghnā grahaṇīpāṇḍurogārśaḥkuṣṭhanāśanāḥ
                        |54| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="278" n="194a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.54cd"><l> vyoṣaṃ trijātakaṃ mustā viḍaṅgāmalake tathā
                        ||54|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.55"><l> navaitāni samāṃśāni trivṛdaṣṭaguṇāni vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         ślakṣṇacūrṇīkṛtān īha dantībhāgadvayaṃ tathā ||55||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.56ab"><l> sarvāṇi cūrṇitānīha gālitāni vimiśrayet
                        |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.56cd"><l> ṣaḍbhiś ca śarkarābhāgair
                        īṣatsaindhavamākṣikaiḥ ||56|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.57ab"><l> piṇḍitaṃ bhakṣayitvā tu tataḥ śītāmbu
                        pāyayet |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.57cd"><l> bastiruktṛḍjvaracchardiśoṣapāṇḍubhramāpaham
                        ||57||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.58ab"><l> niryantraṇamidaṃ sarvaviṣaghnaṃ tu virecanam
                        | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.58cd"><l> trivṛdaṣṭakasañjño 'yaṃ praśastaḥ
                        pittarogiṇām ||58||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.59"><l> bhakṣyaḥ kṣīrānupāno vā pittaśleṣmāturair
                        naraiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhakṣyarūpasadharmatvād āḍhyeṣveva vidhīyate ||59|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.60"><l> tilvakasya tvacaṃ bāhyām antarvalkavivarjitām
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇayitvā tu dvau bhāgau tatkaṣāyeṇa gālayet ||60|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.61"><l> tṛtīyaṃ bhāvitaṃ tena bhāgaṃ śuṣkaṃ tu
                        bhāvitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa trivṛdvatsamprayojayet ||61|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.62"><l> vidhānaṃ tvakṣu nirdiṣṭaṃ phalānāmatha
                        vakṣyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         harītakyāḥ phalaṃ tv asthivimuktaṃ doṣavarjitam ||62||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.63"><l> yojyaṃ trivṛdvidhānena sarvavyādhinibarhaṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         rasāyanaṃ paraṃ medhyaṃ duṣṭāntarvraṇaśodhanam ||63||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.64"><l> harītakī viḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ nāgaraṃ trivṛt |
                        <caesura/>
                         maricāni ca tat sarvaṃ gomutreṇa virecanam ||64||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.65"><l> harītakī bhadradāru kuṣṭhaṃ pūgaphalaṃ tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         saindhavaṃ śṛṅgaveraṃ ca gomūtreṇa virecanam ||65||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.66"><l> nīlinīphalacūrṇaṃ tu nāgarābhayayos tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         lihyād guḍena salilaṃ paścād uṣṇaṃ piben naraḥ ||66|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.67"><l> pippalyādikaṣāyeṇa pibet piṣṭāṃ harītakīm |
                        <caesura/>
                         saindhavopahitāṃ sadya eṣa yogo virecayet ||67||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.68"><l> harītakī bhakṣyamāṇā nāgareṇa guḍena vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         saindhavopahitā vā'pi sātatyenāgnidīpanī ||68||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.69"><l> vātānulomanī vṛṣyā cendriyāṇāṃ prasādanī |
                        <caesura/>
                         santarpaṇakṛtān rogān prāyo hanti harītakī ||69|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.70"><l> śītam āmalakaṃ rūkṣaṃ pittamedaḥkaphāpaham |
                        <caesura/>
                         bibhītakam anuṣṇaṃ tu kaphapittanibarhaṇam ||70|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.71ab"><l> trīṇyapy amlakaṣāyāṇi satiktamadhurāṇi ca
                        |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.71cd"><l> triphalā sarvarogaghnī
                        tribhāgaghṛtamūrcchitā ||71||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.72ab"><l> vayasaḥ sthāpanaṃ cāpi kuryāt santatasevitā
                        |72| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.72cd"><l> harītakīvidhānena phalāny evaṃ prayojayet
                        ||72|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.73"><l> virecanāni sarvāṇi viśeṣāccaturaṅgulāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         phalaṃ kāle samuddhṛtya sikatāyāṃ nidhāpayet ||73|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.74"><l> saptāham ātape śuṣkam ato majjānam uddharet |
                        <caesura/>
                         tailaṃ grāhyaṃ jale paktvā tilavad vā prapīḍya ca ||74|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.75"><l> tasyopayogo bālānāṃ yāvad varṣāṇi dvādaśa |
                        <caesura/>
                         lihyād eraṇḍatailena kuṣṭhatrikaṭukānvitam ||75|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.76"><l> sukhodakaṃ cānupibedeṣa yogo virecayet
                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="279" n="195a"/> eraṇḍatailaṃ triphalākvāthena triguṇena                         tu ||76||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.77"><l> yuktaṃ pītaṃ tathā kṣīrarasābhyāṃ tu virecayet
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         bālavṛddhakṣatakṣīṇasukumāreṣu yojitam ||77||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.78"><l> phalānāṃ vidhiruddiṣṭaḥ kṣīrāṇāṃ śṛṇu suśruta!
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         virecanānāṃ tīkṣṇānāṃ payaḥ saudhaṃ paraṃ matam ||78||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.79"><l> ajñaprayuktaṃ tad dhanti viṣavat
                        karmavibhramāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         vijānatā prayuktaṃ tu mahāntam api saṃcayam || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.80"><l> bhinatty āśveva doṣāṇāṃ rogān hanti ca
                        dustarān
                        <caesura/>
                         mahatyāḥ pañcamūlyās tu bṛhatyoś caikaśaḥ pṛthak ||80||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.81"><l> kaṣāyaiḥ samabhāgaṃ tu tadaṅgāreṣu śoṣitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         amlādibhiḥ pūrvavat tu prayojyaṃ kolasammitam ||81|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.82"><l> mahāvṛkṣapayaḥ pītair yavāgūs taṇḍulaiḥ kṛtā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pītā virecayaty āśu guḍenotkārikā kṛtā ||82||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.83"><l> leho vā sādhitaḥ samyak snuhīkṣīrapayoghṛtaiḥ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhāvitās tu snuhīkṣīre pippalyo lavaṇānvitāḥ ||83|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.84ab"><l> cūrṇaṃ kāmpillakaṃ vā'pi tatpītaṃ
                        guṭikīkṛtam</l></lg>

                    <pb facs="279" n="195b"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.84cd"><l> saptalā śaṅkhinī dantī trivṛdāragvadhaṃ
                        gavām ||84|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.85"><l> mūtreṇāplāvya saptāhaṃ snuhīkṣīre tataḥ param
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kīrṇaṃ tenaiva cūrṇena mālyaṃ vasanam eva ca ||85|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.86ab"><l> āghrāyāvṛtya vā samyaṅ mṛdukoṣṭho viricyate
                        | </l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.86cd"><l> kṣīratvakphalamūlānāṃ vidhānaiḥ
                        parikīrtitaiḥ | </l></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.86ef"><l> avekṣya samyag rogādīn yathāvad upayojayet
                        ||86|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.87"><l> trivṛcchāṇā mitāstisrastisraś ca
                        triphalātvacaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viḍaṅgapippalīkṣāraśāṇāstisraś ca cūrṇitāḥ ||87|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.88"><l> lihyāt sarpirmadhubhyāṃ ca modakaṃ vā guḍena
                        vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhakṣayen niṣparīhārametacchreṣṭhaṃ virecanam ||88|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.89"><l> gulmaṃ plīhodaraṃ kāsaṃ halīmakamarocakam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaphavātakṛtāṃś cānyān vyādhīn etad vyapohati ||89|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.90"><l> ghṛteṣu taileṣu payaḥsu cāpi madyeṣu mūtreṣu
                        tathā raseṣu |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhakṣyānnalehyeṣu ca teṣu teṣu virecanāny agramatir vidadhyāt
                        ||90|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.91"><l> kṣīraṃ rasaḥ kalkamatho kaṣāyaḥ śṛtaś ca śītaś
                        ca tathaiva cūrṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         kalpāḥ ṣaḍete khalu bheṣajānāṃ yathottaraṃ te laghavaḥ pradiṣṭāḥ
                        ||91|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.trailer"><l>iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ sūtrasthāne
                        virecanadravyavikalpavijñānīyo nāma catuś catvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ
                        ||44|| </l></lg>
                </div>


                <div n="45" type="adhyāya">

                      <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.1"><l><pb facs="280" n="196a"/> athāto                         dravadravyavidhim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.3"><l> pānīyam āntarīkṣam anirdeśyarasam amṛtaṃ
                        jīvanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ dhāraṇam āśvāsajananaṃ
                        śramaklamapipāsāmadamūrcchātandrānidrādāhapraśamanam ekāntataḥ
                        pathyatamaṃ ca ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.4"><l> tad evāvanipatitam anyatamaṃ rasam upalabhate
                        sthānaviśeṣān
                        nadīnadasarastaḍāgavāpīkūpacuṇṭīprasravaṇodbhidavikirakedārapalvalādiṣu
                        sthāneṣv avasthitam iti ||4|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.5"><l> tatra lohitakapilapāṇḍunīlapītaśukleṣv
                        avanipradeśeṣu madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyāṇi yathāsaṅkhyam
                        udakāni sambhavanti’ ity eke bhāṣante ||5|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.6"><l> tat tu na samyak pṛthivyādīnām
                        anyonyānupraveśakṛtaḥ salilaraso bhavaty utkarṣāpakarṣeṇa | tatra,
                        svalakṣaṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ bhūmāv amlaṃ lavaṇaṃ ca
                        ambuguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ madhuraṃ; tejoguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ kaṭukaṃ tiktaṃ
                        ca vāyuguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ kaṣāyam; ākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyām
                        avyaktarasam, avyaktaṃ hy ākāśam ity ataḥ; tat pradhānam
                        avyaktarasatvāt tat peyam āntarīkṣālābhe ||6|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="281" n="197a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.7"><l> tatrāntarīkṣaṃ caturvidham | tad yathā dhāraṃ,
                        kāraṃ, tauṣāraṃ, haimam iti | teṣāṃ dhāraṃ pradhānaṃ, laghutvāt ;
                        tat punardvividhaṃ- gāṅgaṃ, sāmudraṃ ceti | tatra gāṅgamāśvayuje
                        māsi prāyaśo varṣati | tayor dvayor api parīkṣaṇaṃ kurvīta-
                        śālyodanapiṇḍamakuthitamavidagdhaṃ rajatabhājanopahitaṃ varṣati
                        deve bahiṣkurvīta, sa yadi muhūrtaṃ sthitastādṛśa eva bhavati tadā
                        gāṅgaṃ patatītyavagantavyaṃ; varṇānyatve sikthapraklede ca
                        sāmudram iti vidyāt, tannopādeyam | sāmudramapyāśvayuje māsi
                        gṛhītaṃ gāṅgavadbhavati | gāṅgaṃ punaḥ pradhānaṃ, tad
                        upādadītāśvayuje māsi | śuciśuklavitatapaṭaikadeśacyutam athavā
                        harmyatalaparibhraṣṭamanyair vā śucibhir bhājanair gṛhītaṃ
                        sauvarṇe rājate mṛnmaye vā pātre nidadhyāt | tat sarvakālam
                        upayuñjīta, tasyālābhe bhaumam | tac cākāśaguṇabahulam | tat punaḥ
                        saptavidham | tad yathā kaupaṃ, nādeyaṃ, sārasaṃ, tāḍāgaṃ,
                        prāsravaṇam, audbhidaṃ, cauṇṭyam iti ||7|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.8"><l> tatra varṣāsvāntarikṣamaudbhidaṃ vā seveta,
                        mahāguṇatvāt ; śaradi sarvaṃ, prasannatvāt hemante sārasaṃ tāḍāgaṃ
                        vā; vasante kaupaṃ prāsravaṇaṃ vā; grīṣme 'pyevaṃ; prāvṛṣi
                        cauṇṭyamanabhivṛṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ ceti ||8|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.9"><l> kīṭamūtrapurīṣāṇḍaśavakothapradūṣitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛṇaparṇotkarayutaṃ kaluṣaṃ viṣasaṃyutam ||9|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.10"><l> yo 'vagāheta varṣāsu pibed vā'pi navaṃ jalam |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa bāhyābhyantarān rogān prāpnuyāt kṣipram eva tu ||10|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.11"><l> tatra yat
                        paṅkaśaivalahaṭhatṛṇapadmapatraprabhṛtibhir avacchannaṃ
                        raviśaśikiraṇānilair nābhijuṣṭaṃ gandhavarṇarasopasṛṣṭaṃ
                        tadvyāpannam iti vidyāt | tasya
                        sparśarūparasagandhavīryavipākadoṣāḥ ṣaṭ sambhavanti | tatra,
                        kharatā paicchilyam auṣṇyaṃ dantagrāhitā ca sparśadoṣaḥ,
                        paṅkasikatāśaivālabahuvarṇatā rūpadoṣaḥ; vyaktarasatā rasadoṣaḥ;
                        aniṣṭagandhatā gandhadoṣaḥ; yad upayuktaṃ
                        tṛṣṇāgauravaśūlakaphaprasekān āpādayati sa viryadoṣaḥ yad

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.71"><l> vātamarśāṃsi kuṣṭhāni kṛmīn hanty udarāṇi ca
                        <caesura/>
                         kopanaṃ kaphavātānāṃ durnāmnāṃ cāvikaṃ dadhi ||71|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.132"><l> atha madhuvargaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         madhu tu madhuraṃ kaṣāyānurasaṃ rūkṣaṃ śītamagnidīpanaṃ varṇyaṃ
                        svaryaṃ laghu sukumāraṃ lekhanaṃ hṛdyaṃ vājīkaraṇaṃ sandhānaṃ
                        śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ(saṅgrāhi ) cakṣuṣyaṃ prasādanaṃ sūkṣmamārgānusāri
                        pittaśleṣmamedomehahikkāśvāsakāsātisāraccharditṛṣṇākṛmiviṣapraśamanaṃ
                        hlādi tridoṣapraśamanaṃ ca; tat tu laghutvāt kaphaghnaṃ,
                        paicchilyān mādhuryāt kaṣāyabhāvāc ca vātapittaghnam ||132|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.139"><l> auddālakaṃ rucikaraṃ svaryaṃ kuṣṭhaviṣāpaham

                        <caesura/>
                         kaṣāyamamlamuṣṇaṃ ca pittakṛt kaṭupāki ca ||139|| </l></lg>

                <div n="46" type="adhyāya">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.1"><l> athāto 'nnapānavidhim adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.2"><l> yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.3"><l> dhanvantarim abhivādya suśruta uvāca-
                        prāgabhihitaḥ ‘prāṇināṃ punarmūlamāhāro balavarṇaujasāṃ ca, sa
                        ṣaṭsu raseṣvāyattaḥ, rasāḥ punardravyāśrayiṇaḥ,’
                        dravyarasaguṇavīryavipākanimitte ca kṣayavṛddhī doṣāṇāṃ sāmyaṃ ca,
                        brahmāderapi ca lokasyāhāraḥ sthity utpattivināśahetuḥ,
                        āhārādevābhivṛddhirbalamārogyaṃ varṇendriyaprasādaś ca, tathā
                        hyāhāravaiṣamyādasvāsthyaṃ; tasyāśitapītalīḍhakhāditasya
                        nānādravyātmakasyānekavidhavikalpasyānekavidhaprabhāvasya pṛthak
                        pṛthagdravyarasaguṇavīryavipākakarmāṇīcchāmi jñātuṃ, na hy
                        anavabuddhasvabhāvā bhiṣajaḥ svasthānuvṛttiṃ roganigrahaṇaṃ ca
                        kartuṃ samarthāḥ; āhārāyattāś ca sarvaprāṇino yasmāttasmād
                        annapānavidhim upadiśatu me bhagavānity uktaḥ provāca bhagavān
                        dhanvantariḥ- atha khalu vatsa suśruta! yathāpraśnamucyamānam
                        upadhārayasva- ||3|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.4"><l> tatra,
                        lohitaśālikalamakardamakapāṇḍukasugandhakaśakunāhṛtapuṣpāṇḍakapuṇḍarīkamahāśāliśītabhīrukarodhrapuṣpaka-
                        dīrghaśūkakāñcanakamahiṣamahāśūkahāyanakadūṣakamahādūṣakaprabhṛtayaḥ
                        śālayaḥ ||4|| </l></lg>

                         pāke rase cāpi kaṭuḥ pradiṣṭaḥ siddhārthakaḥ śoṇitapittakopī <pb facs="303" n="219a"/> tīkṣṇoṣṇarūkṣaḥ kaphamārutaghnas
                        tathāguṇaś cāsitasarṣapo 'pi || 49 || </l></lg>

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.53"><l> atha māṃsavargaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ata ūrdhvaṃ māṃsavargānupadekṣyāmaḥ; tad yathā- jaleśayā, ānūpā,
                        grāmyāḥ, kravyabhuja, ekaśaphā, jāṅgalāśceti ṣaṇmāṃsavargā
                        bhavanti | eteṣāṃ vargāṇāmuttarottarā pradhānatamāḥ | te
                        punardvividhā jāṅgalā ānūpāśceti | tatra jāṅgalavargo 'ṣṭavidhaḥ |
                        tad yathā- jaṅghālā, viṣkirāḥ, pratudā, guhāśayāḥ, prasahāḥ,
                        parṇamṛgā, bileśayā, grāmyāśceti | teṣāṃ jaṅghālaviṣkirau
                        pradhānatamau ||53|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.54"><l>
                        tāveṇahariṇarkṣakuraṅgakarālakṛtamālaśarabhaśvadaṃṣṭrāpṛṣatacāruṣkaramṛgamātṛkāprabhṛtayo
                        jaṅghālā mṛgāḥ kaṣāyā madhurā laghavo vātapittaharāstīkṣṇā hṛdyā
                        bastiśodhanāś ca ||54|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.55"><l> kaṣāyamadhuro hṛdyaḥ pittāsṛkkapharogahā |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.56"><l> madhuro madhuraḥ pāke doṣaghno 'naladīpanaḥ |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.57ab"><l> eṇaḥ kṛṣṇastayor jñeyo hariṇastāmra ucyate | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.57cd"><l> yo na kṛṣṇo na tāmraś ca kuraṅgaḥ so
                        'bhidhīyate ||57|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.58"><l> śītā'sṛkpittaśamanī vijñeyā mṛgamātṛkā |
                        <caesura/>
                         sannipātakṣayaśvāsakāsahikkārucipraṇut ||58|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.59"><l>
                        lāvatittirikapiñjalavartīravartikāvartakanaptṛkāvārtīkacakorakalaviṅkamayūrakrakaropacakra-
                        kukkuṭasāraṅgaśatapatrakutittirikuruvāhakayavālakaprabhṛtayastryāhalā
                        viṣkirāḥ ||59|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.134"><l> atīva rūkṣaṃ māṃsaṃ tu vihaṅgānāṃ phalāśinām

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.135"><l> matsyāśināṃ pittakaraṃ vātaghnaṃ

                        <caesura/>
                        <pb facs="310" n="226a"/> jalajānūpajā grāmyā kracyādaikaśaphās                         tathā ||135|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.136"><l> prasahā bilavāsāś ca ye ca jaṅghālasañjñitāḥ

                        <caesura/>
                         pratudā viṣkirāś caiva laghavaḥ syur yathottaram |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.137"><l> prāmāṇādhikās tu svajātāvalpasārā guravaś ca
                        | sarvaprāṇināṃ sarvaśarīrebhyaḥ pradhānatamā bhavanti
                        yakṛtpradeśavartinastānādadīta; pradhānālābhe madhyamavayaskaṃ
                        sadyaskamakliṣṭamupādeyaṃ māṃsam iti ||137|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.138"><l> bhavati cātra

                        <caesura/>
                         vidāhino vātakarāḥ sakaṣāyā virūkṣaṇāḥ ||292|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.293"><l> udbhidāni palālekṣukarīṣaveṇukṣitijāni |
                        tatra palālajātaṃ madhuraṃ madhuravipākaṃ rūkṣaṃ doṣapraśamanaṃ ca
                        | ikṣujaṃ madhuraṃ kaṣāyānurasaṃ kaṭupākaṃ śītalaṃ ca |
                        tadvadevoṣṇaṃ kārīṣaṃ kaṣāyaṃ vātakopanaṃ ca, (veṇujātaṃ kaṣāyaṃ
                        vātakopanaṃ ca,) bhūmijaṃ guru nātivātalaṃ bhūmitaś cāsyānurasaḥ
                        ||293|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.294"><l> piṇyākatilakalkasthūṇikāśuṣkaśākāni
                        sarvadoṣaprakopaṇāni ||294|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.433"><l> tatra pūrvaśasyajātānāṃ badarāmlaṃ,
                        vaidalānāṃ dhānyāmlaṃ, jaṅghālānāṃ dhanvajānāṃ ca pippalyāsavaḥ,
                        viṣkirāṇāṃ kolabadarāsavaḥ, pratudānāṃ kṣīravṛkṣāsavaḥ,
                        guhāśayānāṃ kharjūranālikerāsavaḥ, prasahānāmaśvagandhāsavaḥ,
                        parṇamṛgāṇāṃ kṛṣṇagandhāsavaḥ, bileśayānāṃ phalāsavaḥ ,
                        ekaśaphānāṃ triphalāsavaḥ, anekaśaphānāṃ khadirāsavaḥ, kūlacarāṇāṃ
                        śṛṅgāṭakakaśerukāsavaḥ, kośavāsināṃ pādināṃ ca sa eva,
                        plavānāmikṣurasāsavaḥ, nādeyānāṃ matsyānāṃ mṛṇālāsavaḥ, sāmudrāṇāṃ
                        mātuluṅgāsavaḥ, amlānāṃ phalānāṃ padmotpalakandāsavaḥ, kaṣāyāṇāṃ
                        dāḍimavetrāsavaḥ, madhurāṇāṃ trikaṭukayuktaḥ khaṇḍāsavaḥ,
                        tālaphalādīnāṃ dhānyāmlaṃ, kaṭukānāṃ dūrvānalavetrāsavaḥ,
                        pippalyādīnāṃ śvadaṃṣṭrāvasukāsavaḥ, kūṣmāṇḍādīnāṃ
                        dārvīkarīrāsavaḥ, cuccuprabhṛtīnāṃ lodhrāsavaḥ, jīvantyādīnāṃ
                        triphalāsavaḥ, kusumbhaśākasya sa eva, maṇḍūkaparṇyādīnāṃ
                        mahāpañcamūlāsavaḥ, tālamastakādīnāmamlaphalāsavaḥ, saindhavādīnāṃ
                        surāsava āranālaṃ ca, toyaṃ vā sarvatreti ||433|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.434"><l> bhavanti cātra-
                        <caesura/>
                         sarveṣām anupānānāṃ māhendraṃ toyam uttamam
                        <caesura/>

                      <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.438"><l> balyaṃ varṇakaraṃ samyaganupānaṃ sadocyate |
                        <caesura/>

                        sthitam |
                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.443"><l> gurulāghavacinteyaṃ svabhāvaṃ nātivartate |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.444"><l> mandakarmānalārogyāḥ sukumārāḥ sukhocitāḥ |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.445"><l> balinaḥ kharabhakṣyā ye ye ca dīptāgnayo
                        narāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         karmanity āś ca ye teṣāṃ nāvaśyaṃ parikīrtyate ||445||
                        <caesura/>
                         ity anupānavargaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.446"><l> athāhāravidhiṃ vatsa! vistareṇākhilaṃ śṛṇu
                        <caesura/>

                        <caesura/>
                         samyagvipakvo niḥsāra āhāraḥ paribṛṃhayet ||527|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.528"><l> viṇmūtramāhāramalaḥ sāraḥ prāgīrito rasaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa tu vyānena vikṣiptaḥ sarvān dhātūn pratarpayet ||528|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.529"><l> kaphaḥ pittaṃ malaḥ kheṣu svedaḥ
                        syānnakharoma ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         netraviṭ tvakṣu ca sneho dhātūnāṃ kramaśo malāḥ ||529|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.530"><l> divā vibuddhe hṛdaye jāgrataḥ puṇḍarīkavat |
                        <caesura/>
                         annamaklinnadhātutvād ajīrṇe 'pi hitaṃ niśi ||530|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.531"><l> hṛdi sammīlite rātrau prasuptasya viśeṣataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         klinnavisrastadhātutvād ajīrṇe na hitaṃ divā ||531|| </l></lg>

                    <pb facs="338" n="257a"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532"><l> imaṃ vidhiṃ yo 'numataṃ
                        mahāmunernṛparṣimukhyasya paṭheddhi yatnataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa bhūmipālāya vidhātumauṣadhaṃ mahātmanāṃ cārhati sūrisattamaḥ
                        ||532|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.trailer"><l> iti bhagavatā śrīdhanvantariṇopadiṣṭāyāṃ
                        tacchiṣyeṇa maharṣiṇā suśrutena viracitāyāṃ suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ
                        sūtrasthāne ṣaṭcatvāriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||46|| </l></lg>
                </div>
            </div>
        </body>
    </text>
